### [1] [SecRed: A program for sector-wise IBP reduction of Feynman integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03703)
*Ruichang Niu,Chao Zeng,Bin Gong,Jianxiong Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: SecRed 是一个用于通过分部积分（IBP）恒等式约化多圈费曼积分的 C++ 程序，采用分扇区处理、自适应种子策略、子扇区过滤及对称性扩展优化，并结合有限域重建与系数无关消元计划，显著提升大规模积分约化的内存效率与并行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IBP约化方法在处理大规模多圈费曼积分（如四环或非平面两环五点）时面临内存爆炸和计算瓶颈，尤其当全局构建方程系统超出单节点内存限制。

Method: 采用扇区逐级约化策略；引入自适应种子盒（含角截断）、可选种子截断、子扇区级过滤器；仅从各扇区主积分展开对称关系；全局阶段用FiniteFlow或FireFly进行有限域系数重建，并基于预记录的系数无关消元顺序在多个素数上重放消元过程，辅以‘头得分’方程排序减少填充。

Result: 成功应用于两环五点非平面族和四环割图族，在不超出单节点内存前提下完成约化；验证了其在内存受限与并行环境下的有效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: SecRed 通过结构化扇区处理、算法定制与有限域高效重建，为高阶微扰计算中大规模IBP约化提供了实用、鲁棒且可扩展的新工具。

Abstract: SecRed is a C++ program for the reduction of multi-loop Feynman integrals with integration-by-parts (IBP) identities. It reduces an integral family sector by sector for memory control and parallelism, and adds algorithms designed for that setting: an adaptive seed box with a corner cut, an optional seed truncation, a subsector level filter, and symmetry relations expanded only from the master integrals of a sector. A global stage assembles the retained equations and performs the finite-field reconstruction of the coefficients using FiniteFlow or FireFly, repeating the elimination over a sequence of primes. On the elimination side, a coefficient-free plan records the elimination order once and is replayed at each prime, so that only the arithmetic is repeated, and a head score orders the equations to reduce fill-in during the elimination. The program is demonstrated on a two-loop five-point non-planar family and on a four-loop cut family, where assembling the global equation system as a whole would exceed the memory of a single node.

</details>


### [2] [Native transverse-momentum evolution and matching for TMD factorization and extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03712)
*Dustin Keller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种原生横动量实现的Collins/CSS2领头幂次横动量依赖（TMD）因子化框架，整合了固定阶分布匹配、原点接触、未展开双尺度演化及可微结构界面，具备严格误差控制与高精度物理验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服传统TMD因子化在横动量区域的理论不自洽、红外敏感及数值实现困难等问题，需构建一个严格、可微、端点无关且具明确红外补全的物理实现框架。

Method: 基于原生横动量变量构建TMD因子化；引入端点无关的规范尺度与红外补全定义Collins–Soper核；采用可微接口连接非微扰输入；通过resolvent卷积或椭圆求解实现动量空间构造；严格使用四环beta/cusp与三环非cusp/Collins–Soper系数，并设定严格NLO硬散射/算符乘积展开边界。

Result: 实现了完整规范腿的有界构造与动量空间可计算性；给出接触项与有限bin误差界、母函数到动量边界的映射、味序递推关系；建立N³LL演化的四指数塔全阶论证；非高斯连续谱检验与三尺度PDF比对验证各模块精度；物理bin偏差低于峰值参考bin的4.27×10⁻⁵；完成质子-质子Drell-Yan与神经闭包初步演示。

Conclusion: 该框架为TMD物理提供了首个具备严格数学结构、可控系统误差、高阶演化完备性及可逆应用潜力的原生横动量实现，但尚未完成N³LL′谱的完整实现与全局PDF抽取。

Abstract: We present a native transverse-momentum realization of the Collins/CSS2 leading-power transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) factorization framework. The construction connects fixed-order distributional matching, origin contacts, unexpanded two-scale evolution, and a differentiable interface for hadronic structure. An endpoint-independent canonical scale with a specified infrared completion generates a Collins--Soper kernel on a common invariant domain. Complete canonical legs are bounded and admit constructive momentum-space evaluation through resolvent convolutions or elliptic solves. We give contact and finite-bin error bounds, parent-to-momentum-boundary translations, ordered flavor recurrences, and an all-order argument for the four exponent towers of $N^3\mathrm{LL}$ evolution. Localized joint-$(x,k_T)$ inputs, nonperturbative rapidity broadening, regulated moments and the full-spectrum subtraction are formulated as distinct parts of the prescription. The physical realization uses sourced four-loop beta/cusp and three-loop noncusp/Collins--Soper ingredients with strict-NLO hard/OPE boundaries. Non-Gaussian continuum tests and three-scale physical-PDF comparisons validate ordinary legs, contacts, individual insertions and analytic input derivatives. Including a declared bound on the continuation above a finite PDF table, physical bin discrepancies remain below $4.27\times10^{-5}$ of the peak reference bin. A qualified proton--proton Drell--Yan and neural-closure demonstration connects the method to inverse use. Higher-order boundary assembly and matched observables are specified without claiming an implemented $N^3\mathrm{LL}'$ spectrum or a completed global extraction.

</details>


### [3] [Topological Diagnostics of CP Degeneracies in Long-Baseline Neutrino Oscillations with Non-Standard Interactions via Pancharatnam Open-Path Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03748)
*Neda Razzaghi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展开放路径Pancharatnam几何相位框架，研究中性流非标准相互作用（NSI）在长基线中微子振荡中的拓扑特征，发现复NSI相位会在几何相位中引入能量依赖的色散，并利用CP共轭下物质势符号翻转导致的中微子/反中微子简并带非平行相交，从而唯一确定CP相位δ_CP与NSI相位φ_eτ，有效打破参数简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长基线中微子实验中由非标准相互作用（NSI）引起的参数简并问题，特别是δ_CP与NSI相位φ_eτ之间的强相关性导致的传统测量难以唯一确定物理参数。

Method: 扩展开放路径Pancharatnam几何相位框架，将含复相位的NSI耦合ε_eτ嵌入DUNE基线（1300 km）分层物质哈密顿量中，计算ν_μ→ν_e和ν̄_μ→ν̄_e通道在0.5–8.0 GeV能谱上的态演化；定义基于干涉项ΔΓ_nm的规范不变几何相位Φ_g^(n)及其CP共轭，构造CP破坏几何相位差ΔΦ_g^CP；分析其在(δ_CP, φ_eτ)平面上的简并带拓扑结构。

Result: 发现中微子与反中微子的几何相位简并带在(δ_CP, φ_eτ)平面以非平行角度相交，形成具有非平凡拓扑结构的唯一交点；该特征对实际探测器能量分辨（calorimetric smearing）鲁棒，可独立、稳健地解除NSI导致的参数简并。

Conclusion: NSI复相位通过几何相位在能谱上留下可识别的拓扑印记，利用CP对称性破缺与物质效应的协同，可在不依赖传统振荡概率拟合的前提下，提供一种新型、本征的参数简并破解方法，提升DUNE等实验对新物理的甄别能力。

Abstract: We investigate the topological signatures of neutral-current Non-Standard Interactions (NSI) in long-baseline neutrino oscillations by extending the open-path Pancharatnam geometric phase framework. Incorporating off-diagonal vector couplings ($\varepsilon_{eτ} = |\varepsilon_{eτ}|e^{iφ_{eτ}}$) into the discrete-layer matter Hamiltonian across the DUNE baseline ($L = 1300\text{ km}$), we analyze the state evolution for both neutrino ($ν_μ\to ν_e$) and antineutrino ($\barν_μ\to \barν_e$) appearance channels over the broadband energy spectrum $E_ν\in [0.5, 8.0]\text{ GeV}$. Our analysis demonstrates that complex NSI phases imprint distinct, energy-dependent spectral dispersions onto the open-path geometric holonomies $Φ_g^{(n)}$ and their $\mathcal{CP}$-conjugate counterparts. By clarifying that these geometric phases manifest through gauge-invariant interference terms $ΔΓ_{nm}$ rather than asymptotic single-channel measurements, we construct the $\mathcal{CP}$-violating geometric phase differences $ΔΦ_g^{\mathrm{CP}}$. We show that the interplay between these geometric dispersions and the matter-potential sign reversal under $\mathcal{CP}$-conjugation forces the neutrino and antineutrino degeneracy bands to intersect at non-parallel angles in the two-dimensional $(δ_{\mathrm{CP}}, φ_{eτ})$ parameter space. This non-trivial topological intersection isolates a unique physical solution, providing a robust, independent diagnostic capable of breaking persistent parameter degeneracies even under realistic calorimetric energy smearing.

</details>


### [4] [Toponium hadroproduction at next-to-next-to-leading order in NRQCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03782)
*Sven Yannick Klein,Fabio Maltoni,Hua-Sheng Shao,Lukas Simon,Guoxing Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次给出了在NRQCD框架下、零宽度极限中单色荷单态顶夸克偶素强子产生过程的次次领头阶（NNLO）量子色动力学（QCD）预言，核心新结果是赝标量顶夸克偶素产生过程短距系数的NNLO QCD修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高顶夸克偶素产生截面理论预言的精度，减小尺度依赖性等系统不确定性，并纳入电弱与相对论修正、额外产生道及微扰计算的束缚态矩阵元。

Method: 基于非相对论QCD（NRQCD）有效理论，在顶夸克零宽度近似下，计算包含NNLO QCD修正的色单态顶夸克偶素强子产生过程，同时计入电弱修正、相对论修正、多通道贡献及微扰束缚态矩阵元。

Result: 在13 TeV LHC质心能量下，得到总截面为4.87^{+0.63}_{-0.65} pb；NNLO修正将重整化与因子化尺度不确定性降低约四倍至百分之几水平；主导剩余不确定性来自库仑尺度相关的束缚态矩阵元。

Conclusion: NNLO计算显著提升了顶夸克偶素产生理论预言的精度与可靠性，但束缚态矩阵元的库仑尺度依赖仍是主要误差来源，需进一步研究。

Abstract: We present the first next-to-next-to-leading-order (NNLO) predictions for inclusive color-singlet toponium hadroproduction within NRQCD in the zero-width limit for the top quark. The central new result is the NNLO QCD correction to the short-distance coefficient for pseudoscalar toponium production. The predictions include electroweak and relativistic corrections, additional production channels, and perturbatively evaluated bound-state matrix elements. At the Large Hadron Collider proton-proton center-of-mass energy of $13\,\mathrm{TeV}$, we obtain a cross section of $4.87^{+0.63}_{-0.65}\,\mathrm{pb}$, with scale and parametric uncertainties combined. The NNLO corrections reduce the renormalization- and factorization-scale uncertainties by approximately a factor of four relative to NLO, to the few-percent level. The dominant remaining uncertainty arises from the Coulomb-scale dependence of the bound-state matrix elements.

</details>


### [5] [GUT Color-Triplet Around the Corner: The Story of an Overlooked Lightness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03783)
*Gia Dvali,Manuel Ettengruber,Anna Jankowsky,Tongxuan Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的解决大统一理论中二重态-三重态分裂问题的方法，即通过Yukawa耦合而非质量分裂实现，使色三重态Higgs成为TeV尺度的长寿命T粒子，并研究了其在超对称SO(10) GUT框架下的具体实现及现象学后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GUT中要求Higgs色三重态伙伴超重以抑制质子衰变，但本文旨在探索通过Yukawa耦合分裂实现双态-三重态分离的新机制，从而打开低能下探测GUT物理的窗口。

Method: 构建一个显式的引力介导破缺的超对称SO(10) GUT模型，分析超对称破缺引发的重场位移如何产生T粒子与标准模型场之间的微弱耦合，并结合S矩阵和Goldstino论证检验真空稳定性，同时考虑物种尺度截断对低能可观测量的影响。

Result: 发现T粒子寿命取决于gravitino质量尺度：约为1秒或远超宇宙年龄；验证了GUT真空不能具有正能量密度，而Minkowski真空稳定；揭示物种尺度截断显著影响低能观测量对GUT物理的敏感性。

Conclusion: Yukawa耦合分裂机制为GUT提供了可检验的TeV尺度新物理信号；T粒子寿命高度依赖于超对称破缺尺度；理论自洽性（如真空稳定性）与紫外完备性（如物种尺度）对低能现象学有关键约束。

Abstract: In traditional approach to the doublet-triplet splitting problem in grand unified theories (GUTs) it is assumed that color-triplet partner of the Higgs doublet must be superheavy in order not to mediate proton decay. However, it has been proposed [1] that the splitting, instead of the mass sector, can occur among the Yukawa couplings. In this scenario the color-triplet emerges as a long-lived $T$-particle with the mass around the TeV scale. This framework provides a direct low-energy window into GUT physics. In the present paper, we study an explicit supersymmetric $SO(10)$ GUT which realizes this mechanism, including effects of gravity-mediated supersymmetry breaking. We show how the shift of the heavy fields, triggered by supersymmetry-breaking, generates a tiny coupling between the $T$-particle and the Standard Model fields and explore the resulting phenomenology. Depending on the order of gravitino mass in which the coupling emerges, the lifetime of the $T$-particle is either around a second or exceeds the age of the Universe. In our analysis, we validate some earlier constraints on (super)gravity vacua imposed by the $S$-matrix: based on the Goldstino Argument, we show that none of the GUT vacua have positive energy densities, whereas the Minkowski vacua are safe against tunneling. We also highlight the role of the species scale cutoff in sensitivity of the low energy observables towards the GUT physics.

</details>

*Mattia Di Mauro,Caleb Gemmell,Dan Hooper,Fabrizio Vassallo*

TL;DR: 本文研究了暗物质湮灭产生重反核（如反氘核和反氦-3）的新型模型，发现该模型可增强反核通量，提升AMS-02和GAPS实验的可观测性，并给出更接近AMS-02候选事例的反氦/反氘比值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解释AMS-02可能探测到的重反核信号，并克服标准WIMP模型中反氦-3产额过低的问题，探索一种能增强反氘核与反氦-3通量的新暗物质模型。

Method: 构建暗物质湮灭至携带重子数与轻子数的色单态标量中介粒子的模型，结合银河系传播模拟与AMS-02/GAPS探测器灵敏度，计算反核事件数，并与7年AMS-02反质子数据兼容约束参数空间。

Result: 在DM-中介粒子质量平面上存在大片区域可产生AMS-02和GAPS可观测的反氘核通量；反氦-3虽更难探测，但在轻中介粒子与中等质量DM下可达潜在可观测水平，且反氦/反氘事例比约为0.1，优于标准WIMP预测。

Conclusion: 该模型为解释未来或已报道的AMS-02反核候选事例提供了可行机制，尤其在反氦-3探测和事例比率方面具有显著优势，值得进一步实验验证。

Abstract: Cosmic-ray antideuterons and antihelium-3 nuclei are promising probes of dark matter (DM). In light of potential detections of heavy antinuclei at AMS-02, we investigate a recently proposed scenario in which DM annihilates into color-singlet scalars carrying baryon and lepton number, which has been shown to enhance antideuteron and antihelium-3 fluxes relative to antiprotons. Accounting for Galactic propagation and detector sensitivities, we calculate the number of events predicted by our model for parameters that are compatible with the seven-year AMS-02 antiproton data. Broad regions of the DM-mediator mass plane result in observable antideuteron fluxes at AMS-02 and GAPS. While detecting antihelium-3 remains more challenging, potentially detectable fluxes can arise for light mediators and moderately light DM, and in general are enhanced relative to the standard WIMP predictions. Furthermore, we find antihelium-to-antideuteron event ratios of O(0.1), closer to those suggested by the reported AMS-02 candidate events than conventional WIMP predictions.

</details>


### [7] [Shortcomings of the squark simplified model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03794)
*Kaustubh Agashe,Roberto Franceschini,Gian F. Giudice,Andre Lessa,Xing Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出，目前在对撞机分析中广泛使用的标量夸克（squark）简化模型无法准确反映现实的低能超对称模型，并显著低估了强子对撞机上的squark产生率；由此导致当前LHC对squark质量限制被严重低估——实际应为2.6–3.0 TeV，而非通常引用的1.7–1.8 TeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有squark简化模型不能代表真实低能超对称模型，导致对LHC等对撞机上squark产生率和质量限制的误判。

Method: 通过对比简化模型与更现实的低能超对称模型中的squark产生截面，重新评估LHC、HL-LHC及FCC-hh对squark的质量探测能力。

Result: 当前LHC对squark的质量限制应为2.6–3.0 TeV，显著高于简化模型给出的1.7–1.8 TeV；该修正影响未来对撞机探测潜力的评估。

Conclusion: 依赖简化模型会系统性低估squark质量上限，需采用更贴近真实超对称场景的模型进行实验解释与理论预测。

Abstract: We argue that the squark simplified model commonly used in collider analyses fails to represent realistic low-energy supersymmetric models and significantly underestimates squark production rates at hadron colliders. This has important implications for extracting squark mass limits at the LHC and assessing the potential reach of HL-LHC or the FCC-hh. In particular, we find that the current LHC squark mass limit is realistically in the range of 2.6-3.0 TeV, rather than the 1.7-1.8 TeV range that is typically quoted based on the simplified model.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出利用超新星前身星（preSNe）阶段的高温长时间特性，比超新星爆发本身更严格地限制MeV量级带电耦合玻色子（如暗光子、类轴子粒子）的参数空间，尤其在1–3 MeV质量区间，对暗光子耦合的限制可强达两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超新星爆发虽剧烈但短暂，而其前身星阶段温度虽较低却持续更久，有利于产生更多可衰变为正电子的粒子，进而贡献银河系511-keV弥散辐射；同时，前身星等离子体频率更低，减弱了对MeV能区激发的屏蔽，使探测更灵敏。

Method: 通过比较前身星与超新星环境中电离耦合玻色子（如暗光子、ALPs）的产额、传播与衰变行为，结合511-keV伽马射线观测约束，计算其耦合强度上限。

Result: 在1–3 MeV质量范围内，前身星对暗光子的耦合限制比超新星强约两个数量级；对其他电离耦合粒子（如ALPs）也获得类似提升。

Conclusion: 超新星前身星是探测MeV尺度轻新物理的强有力天体实验室，可显著拓展传统超新星约束的参数空间。

Abstract: The years preceding a supernova (SN) can constrain new electrophilic bosons with MeV masses more than the explosion itself. Although cooler than SNe, the massive progenitors maintain consistently high temperatures much longer, injecting more particles whose decays to positrons contribute to the diffuse Galactic 511-keV emission. For dark photons, the increase is exacerbated by the lower plasma frequency compared to SNe, where MeV excitations are screened. We find preSNe bounds on dark photon couplings up to two orders of magnitude stronger than SNe for masses between 1 and 3~MeV, with similar improvements for other electrophilic species such as axion-like particles.

</details>


### [9] [Searching for Long-Lived Dark Scalars with MAPP-1 and MAPP-2 at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03893)
*Shafakat Arifeen,James Pinfold,Pierre-Philippe A. Ouimet,Michael Staelens*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了MoEDAL-MAPP实验在高亮度LHC上对Higgs门户暗标量粒子的95%置信水平背景自由灵敏度预测，该粒子通过稀有B介子衰变和奇异希格斯衰变产生，具有长寿命特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

In this paper, we address this problem by introducing a semiparametric estimation framework. Abstract: JWST has revealed an unexpected abundance of z>10 galaxies and striking spectral diversity. Here, we model bursty star formation histories (SFHs) to explore how they shape this diversity and whether spectroscopy can discriminate between explanations for the UV luminosity function (UVLF) excess. This burstiness naturally reproduces UVLFs and SFR upturn and downturn fractions over z~6-11. With increasing redshift, we predict galaxies in recent upturns (SFR$_3$/SFR$_{50}>5$) increasingly dominate the UVLF bright end, reflecting rising accretion rates and burstier low-mass halos. We find the downturn fraction (SFR$_3$/SFR$_{50}<0.2$) is particularly sensitive to stochasticity: increasing towards faint $M_{UV}$ if burstiness increases in lower mass halos, and evolving with redshift only if stochasticity does. However, the model underpredicts the z~14 UVLF, so we explore two models which match the UVLF: increased burstiness and increased star formation efficiency. From z=10 to 14, at fixed $M_{UV}$, increased burstiness predicts the downturn fraction increases by ~2x and the weak-emitter fraction (H$β$ EW<50Å) by ~1.5x. By contrast, the increased efficiency model predicts a constant downturn fraction and declining weak-emitter fraction. Overall, our results indicate burstiness is important for explaining spectral diversity and evolution at z~6-14, and that spectroscopic samples spanning a broad $M_{UV}$ range can establish its role in the z~14 UVLF excess.

Abstract: The Gaia mission has greatly improved our understanding of the stellar binary population. Nearly all 10Be is produced by GCR interactions; SNRs contribute at most about ten percent of the 10Be inherited by newly formed stars. The 10Be/9Be ratio traces the CR energy density, declining smoothly with galactocentric radius, and because stars form where supernovae inject CRs, their natal gas is CR-enriched roughly fivefold relative to the mean interstellar medium, which one-zone estimates miss. Accounting for a possibly higher Galactic star formation rate 4.6 Gyr ago, inheritance of GCR-produced 10Be can then explain the early Solar System's 10Be/9Be in our models, without late injection or local irradiation. Even when GCR-induced 10Be production in dense gas is reduced tenfold, the meteoritic value is reached in the innermost disc and missed by only a factor of a few elsewhere, so the main conclusion is robust to uncertain low-energy GCR penetration. Abstract: Intermediate-mass black holes (IMBHs, $10^2 < m_{\rm BH}/\mathrm{M}_{\odot} < 10^5$) are elusive objects bridging the gap between stellar-mass and supermassive black holes, whose origin remains uncertain. We investigate the formation of VMSs and IMBH seeds via repeated mergers and collisions as a function of cluster initial conditions, assessing the implications for globular clusters and dense high-redshift system. We develop STARfall, a semi-analytic tool that follows the evolution of a massive stellar binary via binary-single interactions, mergers, collisions, and stellar evolution, while consistently accounting for the host cluster evolution. Our results show that clusters with central densities above $10^6\,\mathrm{M}_{\odot}\,\mathrm{pc}^{-3}$ can undergo a catastrophic sequence of stellar collisions, or "starastrophe", within $3$ Myr. Through this process, VMSs rapidly grow in mass and collapse into IMBHs with masses of up to $10^3-10^4\,\mathrm{M}_{\odot}$, despite the effects of intense stellar winds. Applying our models to observed systems, we find that compact clusters in the Cosmic Gems Arc and Milky Way globular clusters could have hosted VMSs that collapsed into IMBHs in this mass range. Despite their longer core-collapse timescales, the inferred central densities of Little Red Dots may also support IMBH formation in the same mass interval. Our models show that VMS assembly requires two essential ingredients: short core-collapse times and large central densities. Compared to observed systems, our models suggest that clusters in the Cosmic Gems Arcs and several Galactic globular clusters could have nurtured VMSs that directly collapsed to IMBHs with masses $10^3-10^4\,\text{M}_{\odot}$.

Abstract: The baryon fraction provides a fundamental diagnostic of baryonic matter distribution within dark matter halos and the baryon cycle. While its cosmic value is well constrained, the baryon content within halos on nearly all scales is significantly lower than the cosmic mean; this is called the ``missing baryon problem.'' Using the high spatial, mass, and temporal resolutions of the NewCluster simulation, we investigate the baryon fraction evolution by tracing halo assembly histories from merger trees and gas tracer particles. We identify three distinct evolutionary phases: an early growth phase driven by cold accretion in low-mass halos, a suppression phase likely dominated by supernova feedback, and a recovery phase in massive halos enabled by deeper potential wells and hot-mode accretion. We further find that the redshift dependence of baryon fraction, particularly in low-mass halos, is influenced by the thermal state of the intergalactic medium, with suppression emerging after the surrounding gas becomes persistently hotter than the halo virial temperature. In addition, the scatter in baryon fraction at fixed halo mass correlates with the large-scale environment, with baryon-rich halos preferentially residing closer to filamentary structures. Overall, our results highlight that baryon fraction evolution is governed by the interplay between feedback and environment, requiring a time-resolved perspective on the baryon cycle.

The spectrum requires a two-component model: a compact, self-absorbed component with a stratified gigahertz-peaked (GPS)/high-frequency-peaked (HFP) spectral shape (turnover at $9.4^{+0.6}_{-0.5}$ GHz), superposed on an extended, optically thin power-law lobe ($α_{\rm pl} = -0.78$) that dominates below ~1.6 GHz. The two-component structure admits two interpretations that the present data cannot distinguish: the extended emission may be the growing lobe of a genuinely young, expanding radio galaxy, or relic plasma from an earlier episode of a transient or recurrent jet. Under either, H 0557$-$385 is better understood as an intermediate object: optically a Seyfert, but with the radio-physical properties of a newly ignited or recently re-triggered radio galaxy. We model CRRLs across this band, accounting for doppler, collisional, and radiation broadening, and we estimate line strengths using two independent methods - fixing either the peak or the frequency-integrated optical depth - applied both uniformly across the sky and with region-dependent values informed by prior low-frequency observations. We simulate LuSEE-Night's expected response to these CRRLs and further convolve the beam-diluted spectra with LuSEE-Night's polyphase-filter-bank spectrometer response, including its zoomed-mode frequency resolution of $\sim$390 Hz, to obtain the expected observed brightness temperature. Using these simulated spectra, we estimate the integration time required to detect individual CRRLs at SNR=5 across the six sky models considered. Required integration times range from as short as 30 hours (in the most optimistic model where the optical depth across the sky is $\sim$10$^{-3}$) to 27.6 years (in the most conservative models where the optical depth across the sky is $\sim$10$^{-4}$). Stacking the 361 simulated C$α$ lines between 10 and 50 MHz reduces the integration time needed for an SNR=5 detection to $\sim$11 minutes and $\sim$151 hours respectively, indicating that LuSEE-Night has realistic chances of making a stacked detection of C$α$ RRLs within a realistic mission lifetime, even under the most conservative optical-depth assumptions.

Abstract: This article presents an overview of key scientific challenges in the astrophysics and astrochemistry of the interstellar and intergalactic media. Addressing many of these challenges will become possible with the Russian space observatory Spektr-UF, which is currently being prepared for launch. The ultraviolet (UV) spectral range is critically important for studying the evolution of the Universe, as it contains fundamental absorption and emission lines of most chemical elements and the most important molecules. These lines make it possible to reliably determine the physical conditions and chemical composition of various astrophysical objects, as well as the characteristics of the processes occurring within them. The article examines the prospects for UV observations across a broad range of scientific problems, from investigating the epoch of helium reionization and the thermal history of the Universe to mapping ongoing star formation in galaxies. Dedicated sections address the study of the chemical evolution of galaxies at intermediate redshifts, the distribution of "hidden" baryons and metals, the small-scale structure of interstellar clouds, the physics of photodissociation regions and protoplanetary disks, and the modeling of UV emission from nebulae surrounding fast-moving pulsars. The high sensitivity and spectral resolution of the Spektr-UF instruments are expected to provide fundamentally new data across all these research areas, filling existing gaps in our understanding of the interstellar and intergalactic media.

Abstract: We here present the first ever mass-metallicity relation (MZR) of fossil disk populations using the resolved stellar populations in the James Webb Space Telescope Near-Infrared Camera (JWST/NIRCam) images of six nearby galaxies. Both the mass and metallicity were determined using the Red Giant Branch (RGB) stars, employing methods that have been well established to study the halo populations of nearby galaxies using the Hubble Space Telescope images. Specifically, we obtained the mass and metallicity by comparing the color, magnitude and number density near the tip of the RGB (TRGB) stars in the F115W$-$F200W vs F200W color-magnitude diagrams (CMDs) with that of the PARSEC isochrones of 4 and 13~Gyr. The RGB metallicities of the disks of nearby galaxies closely match those found in galaxies at redshifts $z>8$, with the metallicity range following a power-law relation with the fossil disk mass. The power-law index of the fossil disk MZR, $γ\approx0.26$, compares well with the typical value found in MZRs obtained using gas metallicities across redshifts. Abstract: The corotation radius R_CR of a spiral galaxy marks the radius where the angular speed of the spiral pattern matches the orbital speed of the disk material. Building on the color-gradient framework of \citet{Puerari1997}, we extract azimuthal brightness profiles from deprojected dual-band images, fit periodic models to the profiles, and identify corotation candidates at radii where the phase difference between the two stellar tracers changes sign. Each candidate is assigned an adaptive uncertainty interval and reported with a goodness-of-fit metric. We benchmark the method against a cross-method literature compilation, scoring every technique -- including our own -- against a reference built from the published measurements alone and evaluated leave-one-out. Over the $90$ galaxies for which such a reference exists, the pipeline places a correct corotation radius in $80.0\%$ of them, recovers $63.6\%$ of their known corotation radii, and reaches a harmonic mean of recall and purity of $0.58$, the highest values of any method in the comparison. Unlike the published methods, each of which was applied to a small, curated set of galaxies, our pipeline is run on the entire reference sample without any pre-selection, a sample two to six times larger than existing methods. The pipeline is scalable to large surveys, flexible with respect to spiral mode, and naturally recovers multiple corotation radii in galaxies with complex spiral structure, providing a uniform framework for studying disk resonances across diverse galaxy populations.

Abstract: The vertical distribution of molecular gas in the Milky Way disk is poorly studied, especially in the 4th quadrant and the far side of the Galaxy. The vertical distribution around the tangent regions of the spiral arm is fitted with two Gaussian components, the narrow one with an FWHM thickness of 25 pc and the wide one of about 100 pc. Two explanations are proposed: they are two sub-components of the thin disk (the narrowest tracing the spiral arm and the other the inter arms components), or they trace both thin and thick disks that show a smaller thickness when observed with CO 3-2 than when observed with CO 1-0. The latter indicates that the density distribution of the interstellar medium exhibits a steeper gradient at larger heights above the plane. Clumps in the two tangent regions are searched and divided into groups by relative height. Compared with the low relative height clumps ($|z|<50$ pc), the median relative height clumps ($50~\mathrm{pc}<|z|<100~\mathrm{pc}$) have lower excitation temperature and surface density, with fewer high values in the distribution. A similar tentative trend is found for the mass.

Abstract: Satellite dwarf galaxies are reshaped by the tidal field of their host, a process that may account for part of the structural diversity of the observed dwarf population. We study how the stellar component of a satellite responds to tidal stripping using a suite of 480 controlled $N$-body simulations of satellite galaxies consisting of both dark matter (DM) and stars orbiting a Milky-Way-mass host. The simulated dwarfs span a broad range of DM halo structural and stellar-distribution parameters, including halo concentration, inner density slope, stellar-to-halo mass ratio, compactness of the stellar distribution with respect to the halo, as well as the Sérsic index of the stellar profiles. We present a comprehensive library of tidal tracks, namely, the evolution of the stellar mass, half-mass radius, mean surface density, and line-of-sight velocity dispersion, as functions of the bound mass fraction of the hosting DM subhalo, and provide simple and accurate fitting functions. We also identify the dependence of tidal tracks on key factors in the initial structural conditions. Notably, stellar compactness determines the duration over which the stellar mass remains relatively intact while the surrounding DM is being stripped. The Sérsic index and inner DM slope, in turn, govern the late-stage stellar mass-loss slope and the contraction or expansion of the remnant. Our simulations and empirical models provide a framework for modelling the stellar evolution of satellites across a broad range of progenitor structures, useful for semi-analytical models of satellite galaxy evolution.

This orientation-based unified model, however, fails to explain differences in host galaxy properties, such as star formation rates, of Type-1 and Type-2 AGNs. Star-formation histories (SFHs) can shed light on the connection between AGN type and host galaxy properties, but are challenging to extract from Type-1 AGN that outshine their host galaxies. We use mean-field independent component analysis (MFICA) spectrum decomposition to extract the stellar populations of $\sim$3000 optically selected Type-1 and Type-2 AGN host galaxies from the seventh data release (DR7) of the Sloan Digital Sky Survey (SDSS), matched in redshift ($0.16 \leq z \leq 0.4$) and AGN luminosity ($6.5<\log_{10} [L_{[OIII]}/L_\odot] <9.5$). Using MFICA, we find that only $\sim$10 per cent of AGN host galaxies have recently ($\lesssim1$\,Gyr) and rapidly quenched star formation following a starburst, with Type-2 AGN host galaxies having done so at most 100\,Myrs more recently than Type-1 AGN host galaxies. For most ($\sim$90 per cent) AGN hosts with normal recent star formation histories, Type-2 AGNs are associated with slightly younger stellar populations than Type-1 AGNs. The younger stellar populations of Type-2 AGN hosts can be explained by a link between dusty torus covering factors and stellar populations, by host-galaxy obscuration of AGN, or by an evolution in AGN type without major bursts or rapid quenching of star formation.

Abstract: Modern imaging surveys generate data at rates that exceed the throughput of photometry pipelines built on iterative, per-source fitting, a construct that does not map onto parallel hardware. We present a set of algorithms for point-source detection and photometry reformulated so that the entire reduction runs natively on a Graphics Processing Unit (GPU), implemented in GPUPhot. Their design rests on a single principle: every stage is cast as a convolution with a fixed kernel or an element-wise operation on full-frame maps. The algorithms are designed to operate on wide-field images with a spatially variable Point Spread Function (PSF), where the field-dependent PSF is described through a small set of fixed, flux-conserving principal-component eigenmodes with position-dependent coefficients, so that the spatially varying matched filter decomposes exactly into a few genuine convolutions while retaining its maximum-likelihood foundation. We show that, even in fields with strong background gradients and markedly inhomogeneous PSFs, the framework achieves sub-pixel astrometric accuracy and few-millimagnitude photometric precision for bright sources. The pipeline is already in production at the telescopes of the Teide Observatory, where it has processed 3.7 million images since 2023, amounting to 1.2 PB of data, at effective end-to-end rates below 15 s per 150-Mpx image, from ingestion of the raw frame to a source catalog with astrometric and photometric solutions. Because the same primitives underlie every stage, the individual algorithms are self-contained and applicable well beyond photometry. They form the basis of a complete, openly developed software package, released publicly with this paper.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用拉丁超立方体抽样（LHS）及其正交数组增强版本（OA-LHS）进行分层抽样，分别消除单变量和成对变量引起的方差成分。

Abstract: In applied statistics, likelihoods involving high-dimensional integrals are often approximated via Monte Carlo summation. Controlling the resulting numerical variance is crucial for reliable inference. We provide a practical introduction to Latin hypercube sampling (LHS) and its extension based on orthogonal arrays (OA-LHS) as strategies to reduce this variance by stratifying the sampling distribution. LHS asymptotically removes variance contributions from individual coordinates, while strength-2 OA-LHS also removes those from pairwise interactions, yielding orders-of-magnitude improvements over plain Monte Carlo estimators for suitable integrands. As a case study, we investigate their application to the selection function entering hierarchical inference of gravitational-wave populations. At approximately one million injections, we find only a marginal reduction in variance, with low statistical significance. Nevertheless, the computational cost of constructing these designs is negligible relative to evaluating gravitational-wave injections, making even marginal gains worth considering for future applications. This practical introduction to LHS and OA-LHS offers a starting point for other applications in physics and astronomy involving computationally expensive Monte Carlo calculations.

</details>

Conclusion: 跨巡天模态插补是一个新颖且具挑战性的机器学习问题，需专门建模仪器差异与稀有事件分布；COSMIO为该方向提供了首个标准化基准，揭示了当前方法局限，并强调面向天文科学任务驱动的插补模型设计必要性。

Abstract: Large-scale astronomical surveys define a heterogeneous, partially observed multi-survey learning setting: each object is observed by only a subset of surveys, while survey observations differ in resolutions, wavelength coverage, noise characteristics, and footprints. This setting gives rise to a natural machine learning problem: given observations of an object in some surveys, how to infer its appearance in other surveys. We formalize this as the problem of cross-survey modality imputation and introduce COSMIO, a multi-survey benchmark of aligned observations obtained by forward-rendered shared astrophysical scenes across LSST, Euclid, and Roman under survey-specific instrument characteristics. The benchmark enables systematic evaluation of imputation across source-target survey combinations and missing-survey patterns. We evaluate a broad range of machine learning methods for imputation, which reveal a clear domain gap: methods developed for existing imputation settings transfer poorly to the survey setting. We identify rare-event imputation as a central challenge. This regime arises in the early stages of new surveys, when rare but scientifically valuable phenomena may be available only from prior surveys. We use strong gravitational lensing, where light from a background galaxy is gravitationally deflected by a foreground galaxy, as a representative rare-event setting. We evaluate model performance on zero-shot imputation of gravitational lenses and on downstream scientific tasks using the imputed lens observations. On our constructed evaluation set of galaxy-galaxy strong lenses, performance degrades under rare-event distribution shift, and this degradation further propagates to the downstream lens-detection task. Code: https://github.com/fw-ic/COSMIO.

Abstract: The International Liquid Mirror Telescope (ILMT) is a 4-meter survey telescope continuously observing towards the zenith in the SDSS g', r', and i' bands. This survey telescope is designed to detect various astrophysical transients (for example, supernovae) and very faint objects like multiply-imaged quasars and low surface brightness galaxies. Ensuring accurate and stable calibration of supernova (SN) and quasar measurements is essential for studying their intrinsic variability and long-term brightness evolution. In this work, we present a dedicated calibration approach tailored for ILMT point-like object observations obtained in Time Delayed Integration (TDI) mode. Using ZTF reference sources cross-matched with the Pan-STARRS1 catalog, we derive robust photometric zero-points along the ILMT scan. Abstract: This paper presents an automated end-to-end pipeline to detect and characterize artificial satellites and space debris streaks. <details>

Motivation: 在科研规模迅速扩大、项目日益庞大的背景下，作者关注基础物理学研究中的内在物理限制和外部资源限制（行星边界），并反思当前‘贪婪、无节制、开采式’科研模式对研究人员个体和集体的制约。

Abstract: Fundamental physics faces several intrinsic limitations, such as the finite speed of light in vacuum, the absolute zero temperature, Heisenberg's uncertainty principle and so on. At the same time, the material resources necessary to conduct research in astrophysics, such as energy, fresh water, and rareearth elements, are also limited in both quantity and extraction capabilities, a concept often referred to as planetary boundaries. In the context of scientific research growing at a frenetic pace, following the greedy quenchless extractivist model of our era, and which is giving rise to an ever-increasing number of largescale, increasingly ambitious projects: what limits us, individually and collectively, in the practice of our occupation\,? In this conference proceeding, we discuss certain limiting factors we encounter in our daily work, which we need to reflect on collectively in order to move forward, toward a more sustainable and effective research that preserves the well-being of researchers, regardless of their career stage.

</details>

Abstract: Athena++ is a finite-volume astrophysical magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) code. As of writing, isotropic thermal conduction and kinematic viscosity, Ohmic resistivity, and ambipolar diffusion are present in the main branch. Here we present a fork of Athena++, ``Athena-Aniso''. It implements fully anisotropic thermal conduction (parallel, perpendicular, and the Righi--Leduc effect); anisotropic viscosity (all five Braginskii viscosity tensors); the Hall effect; and several thermoelectric effects (Peltier, Seebeck, Nernst, and Ettingshausen). These effects can be weighted by ionisation fraction in a partially ionised gas, with the neutral part diffusing isotropically. We have also added isotropic bulk viscosity. The Biermann battery is not implemented here. The dissipative terms have super-time-stepping support, while the non-dissipative terms are combined with the ideal MHD flux. A CFL condition for all terms is derived and implemented using von Neumann stability analysis. The dissipative terms are found to be stable under first-order time integration. The non-dissipative terms are found to be stable under third-order time integration. We additionally derive new signal speeds for a modified HLLE Riemann solver. These suppress grid-scale non-linear instabilities still present in the non-dissipative terms. The Nernst/Ettingshausen system is found to be ill-posed unless it is stabilised by both perpendicular thermal conduction and perpendicular magnetic diffusion. We derive a well-posedness criterion for this. We provide numerical tests of each implemented effect against derived analytical solutions.

</details>

Abstract: Imaging atmospheric Cherenkov telescopes (IACTs) cameras are constantly subject to signals from the night sky background and electronic noise during event recordings. Image cleaning methods are typically employed to eliminate pixels that are mostly noise-contaminated and lack sufficient Cherenkov light. In this paper, we present the implementation of the optimised next neighbour (ONN) technique to the Very Energetic Radiation Imaging Telescope Array System (VERITAS) reconstruction pipeline. With this method, cleaning thresholds are derived in a two-parameter space, composed of the minimum total integrated charge of a cluster of pixels, $q_{\rm min}$, and by the maximum difference in the pulse arrival time within the cluster, $δ_t$. The cleaning contours in $q_{\rm min} - δ_t$ are adjusted to set a high probability of noise suppression while optimising the retention of pixels containing Cherenkov light. In comparison to the VERITAS double-pass image cleaning method, effective areas are increased at the lower energy range ($\sim$ 60 - 90 GeV) by a factor of up to $\sim$ 25. Energy thresholds are reduced by up to $\sim$ 22\%. Testing the method on Crab Nebula data resulted in an increased number of reconstructed excess events at energies < 1 TeV by a factor of up to $\sim$ 82. Finally, an increase in the differential sensitivity of VERITAS by a factor of up to $\sim$ 8 was observed at $\sim$ 20 GeV. With the addition of BDT-based $γ$/hadron separation cuts, the method improves the detection significance of a 20 hour Crab Nebula dataset, while excess event rates are enhanced by $\sim$ 16\%. In summary, the ONN image cleaning provides a reduced energy threshold and a higher rate of event reconstruction. The technique is especially advantageous for the analysis of gamma-ray sources presenting transient and soft spectra.

</details>

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are now applied to astronomical data analysis. They retrieve the literature, reduce observations, and draft conclusions. Under pressure to complete a task, such agents can fabricate results, and the numbers they report may not come from the analysis they ran. We present auto_research, a semi-autonomous multi-agent pipeline that retrieves and analyses raw data from the High Energy Astrophysics Science Archive Research Center (HEASARC) and writes the analysis tools that it needs. We check the work of the agents at two points. A new tool enters the toolkit after it passes automatic checks, including repeated test runs and a scan for phrases that accompany invented values, and a code review by a model from a different provider; a human decides whether to promote it to the trusted branch. A number in a report passes only if a recorded output of a toolkit tool or the request contains it, or if the report gives its location in a cited paper. We test the pipeline with data from the Imaging X-ray Polarimetry Explorer (IXPE). Of 65 defects that we planted in the toolkit, 47 pass the automatic checks, and one passes the code review as well; the review also rejects 40 of 69 correct changes. The pipeline reproduces the published IXPE results for Cyg X-1: all 14 compared quantities agree within 3 sigma, and the polarization degrees are lower than the published values. With weaker models in the main roles, the pipeline recovers 12 of the 14 quantities and reports the other two as not measured. We also used the pipeline for a polarization-stability analysis of six observations, a survey of polarization against hardness in 29 observations, and a search for a time lag between the Stokes parameters q and u.

Abstract: Prime-Cam is one of the primary instruments being developed for use by the CCAT collaboration with the Fred Young Submillimeter Telescope (FYST), which is undergoing construction at a height of 5600 m in Cerro Chajnantor. Prime-Cam can host up to seven instrument modules with kinetic inductance detectors (KIDs), including broadband modules ranging 280-850 GHz and imaging spectrometers for line-intensity mapping. Prime-Cam requires a number of hardware devices for standard operation and data collection, and so a distributed control system must be developed for software integration of these devices and user-friendliness when controlling Prime-Cam in the field, especially during remote operation that will be typical during observations. The Prime-Cam Control System (PCS) is being developed using the Observatory Control System (OCS) framework originally developed for Simons Observatory (SO) and is adapted for Prime-Cam and CCAT-specific requirements. Within OCS/PCS, hardware devices are controlled individually by long-running software processes known as agents, which are typically deployed in Docker containers running in the background of one of the Prime-Cam network computers. These agents are controlled by client scripts through the command line or frontend tools like Nextline and PCS-web. PCS also makes use of several external open source software tools for remote operations, data management, and logging. This system is being tested in the lab in preparation for deployment in 2026 for use with Prime-Cam on FYST.

Abstract: Reconstruction of anisotropic cosmic birefringence from the CMB is limited by lensing $B$-mode sample variance and partially degenerate with the lensing curl. We study the joint reconstruction of lensing (gradient and curl) and birefringence at the maximum-a-posteriori point, whose spectra are near-optimal statistics. We predict the joint response and noise analytically, validate them on simulations, and show unbiased inference of its amplitude. Errors will shrink $5$-fold relative to quadratic estimators at SPT-3G+ depths, promising much tighter constraints on parity-violating new physics, owing to the removal of the lensing $B$-polarization sample variance.

</details>

Abstract: Tackling cosmology with Milgromian Dynamics (MOND) has been a challenge ever since its theoretical inception. In a hybrid MOND scheme, a hypothesized light sterile neutrino of $\approx$ 11 eV/$c^2$ rest-mass is able to provide realistic initial conditions, fitting the third peak of the Planck CMB power spectrum as a Hot Dark Matter (HDM) component. At the same time, MOND governs the gravitational dynamics of density perturbations, while the background expansion follows the standard FLRW metric. We conduct and analyze hydrodynamical simulations of the fiducial $Λ$CDM model ($H_0 = 67.8$ km/s/Mpc, $Ω_m \approx 0.31$, $Ω_{\text{CDM}} \approx 0.26$), the progenitor $ν$HDM model ($H_0 = 67.64$ km/s/Mpc, $Ω_m = 0.3, Ω_ν\approx 0.26$), and its optimized version (opt-$ν$HDM model, $H_0 \approx 55.6$ km/s/Mpc, $Ω_m \approx 0.49$, $Ω_ν \approx 0.42$), utilizing the publicly available codes RAMSES and Phantom of RAMSES. We compare for the first time the total matter power spectrum $P(k)$ of the hydrodynamical simulations at various redshifts, examining the systematic differences of the three investigated cosmologies. The redshift evolution of the power spectrum of the $Λ$CDM model compared to the $ν$HDM variants' counterpart offers important insight about the onset of structure formation. The combination of a light sterile neutrino and MOND gravity exceeds the $Λ$CDM model's matter $P(k)$ over all collapsing scales at late times ($z\lesssim 1$). The density and velocity fields reveal a distinct difference between the underlying laws of gravity. Regardless of the modeled dependence in the jointly estimated data from Planck, DES Y1, SDSS, and eBOSS, the $ν$HDM hybrid models seem to produce an excess of power at associated resolution scales due to their cosmological design.

</details>

Abstract: Mapping the three-dimensional distribution of dark matter is key to tracing the growth of cosmic structure. The candidate signals are consistent with chance fluctuations in the full search. We find no statistically significant evidence for alignment of structural axes of radio sources in SUMSS images. Abstract: Survey boundaries and nonlinear dynamics complicate reconstruction of large-scale matter-density and peculiar-velocity fields from galaxy redshift surveys. We infer the initial density field using a Gaussian prior and likelihood, with the Zel'dovich model displacing particles from a uniform Lagrangian grid. Both yield maximum a posteriori (MAP) fields; only the linear posterior is sampled. Our main findings are: \textit{(i)} Both reconstructions yield similar large-scale density structures and coherent velocity patterns in the survey interior, beyond roughly \(50\,h^{-1}\,\mathrm{Mpc}\) from the footprint boundaries. \textit{(ii)} Comparison with Cosmicflows-4 groups, predominantly from the SDSS Fundamental Plane sample in the DESI overlap, shows close agreement between mean observed radial velocities and MAP predictions when binned by the latter, despite substantial individual scatter. \textit{(iii)} Both yield similar reflex-dipole profiles, defined as mean shell velocities relative to those at randomly sampled centres, out to \(\sim225\,h^{-1}\,\mathrm{Mpc}\). Sparse sampling limits the largest radii. \textit{(iv)} In an exploratory BAO test, shifting galaxies and randoms to a target redshift \(z=8\) using reconstructed displacements sharpens the acoustic feature in both cases, with a slightly more pronounced peak for Zel'dovich. Method: 基于Horndeski理论框架，要求其弱场极限为折射引力（RG）；将RG中的引力介电常数ε(ρ)等同于Horndeski函数G₄(φ)；推导背景宇宙演化和线性密度扰动增长方程。

Abstract: We study the formation and evolution of the cosmic large-scale structure (LSS) in a scalar-tensor Horndeski theory of gravity without dark matter. We impose that this theory reduces to Refracted Gravity (RG) in the weak-field limit. RG is a non-relativistic theory where the gravitational permittivity $ε(ρ)$, with $ρ$ the baryonic matter density, provides the gravitational boost required to describe both the dynamics of galaxies and galaxy clusters without dark matter. We show that $ε(ρ)$ equals the function $G_4(\varphi)$ of the scalar field $\varphi$ of our Horndeski model. The background evolution of a flat, matter-dominated, homogeneous and isotropic universe shows that $\varphi$ is also responsible for the accelerated expansion of the universe and that the model is capable of reproducing the observed redshift evolution of the Hubble-Lemaître parameter. We finally develop the linear perturbation theory and study the growth factor equation. The model fails to correctly describe the LSS by predicting a late onset of the formation of cosmic structure at redshift $z\lesssim 1$.

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological dynamics of axion-monopole interacting dark energy, examining whether a microscopically motivated dark-sector interaction can sustain stable late-time acceleration. In particular, magnetic monopoles constitute dark matter and an ultralight axion contributes to dark energy, with their interaction arising from the Witten effect through field-dependent dyon masses. We formulate the autonomous system, classify its critical points and stability, and investigate the evolution analytically and numerically. We find that stable de Sitter attractors at finite positive Hubble parameter require a positive cosmological constant and correspond to axion-potential minima, whereas those at maxima are unstable. Nevertheless, transient axion-driven acceleration remains possible without an explicit cosmological constant. Furthermore, the interaction induces matter-era tracking, namely an attracting slow-drift regime rather than an exact critical point. The interaction-effective dark-energy equation of state can cross the phantom divide, although the physical equation of state remains non-phantom. Extending the analysis to the full Bogomol'nyi-Prasad-Sommerfield dyon mass, we find that the main dynamical features persist, while the field evolution and interaction strength depend on the microscopic dyon properties. Our results demonstrate how a microscopically motivated dark-sector interaction can give rise to distinctive cosmological evolution and effective dark-energy behavior.

Abstract: In this work we assess non-Gaussian extensions of Fisher forecasting based on the Derivative Approximation for Likelihoods (DALI). We simulate ensembles of bright siren catalogs for third-generation observatories, adopting a Cosmic Explorer (CE) and a triangular Einstein Telescope (ET) network, frequency-domain inspiral waveforms, and selection-motivated priors for counterpart detection. We reconstruct approximate likelihoods using Fisher and the first DALI correction (Doublet), and propagate the inferred distance information to cosmological inference in a w0waCDM model. We find that the Doublet approximation provides tighter cosmological constraints than the Fisher approximation, reducing the inferred uncertainties by approximately a factor of two in agreement with non-approximate likelihood (called Exact) results. The adopted non-Gaussian GW priors induce a systematic displacement of the inferred luminosity distances from the fiducial values, which is also present in the exact GW reconstruction and propagates into the cosmological parameters. Compared with the exact cosmological likelihood, the Doublet results differ by only ~10% on average in the parameter uncertainties, compared with ~195% for the traditional Fisher approximation. The Doublet approximation also substantially reduces the computational cost, by a factor of ~55 for the GW analysis and by ~12 for cosmological likelihood evaluations, yielding an estimated reduction of ~55 in the total computational cost of the combined analysis. Our results show the potential of DALI-based approximations to provide accurate and computationally efficient tools for forecasting in bright siren cosmology.

With simulation-based knowledge of a handful of electron free parameters, the electron distribution largely acts as a second realization of the matter distribution. Assuming access to localized FRBs with redshifts, we perform cosmological forecasts using these two large-scale matter probes, which have no strong dependence on baryonic feedback models nor on parameters that encode galaxy formation. We show that the parameters $σ_8$ and $Ω_m$ are constrained at a level similar to or better than that obtained by combining FRB dispersion with weak lensing of galaxy shapes under conservative assumptions. These results motivate the use of FRB data as a complementary probe of cosmic structure and will be especially compelling as redshifts become available for localized FRB host galaxies.

Abstract: We examine the linear response of the $\PT$-broken phase of a BRST invariant model whose fermion masses form the complex conjugate pair $m_{\pm}=N\pm iav$. It splits into two channels, kept apart by an exact $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$, and one Euclidean correlator decides both. In the diagonal channel it oscillates in Euclidean time, so that channel admits no positive-norm realization: we exhibit a test function of negative norm and close every repair, the choice of boundary value among them. In the mixed channel the same correlator is completely monotone, which proves the spectral density positive; there we obtain the vacuum conductivity in closed form, with a real threshold at $ω=2N$, and positivity survives at finite temperature below a bound whose onset we give exactly. The longitudinal density of that channel is negative by the square of the mass splitting; an antisymmetric derivative removes it, and the resulting field-strength composite has a positive one-particle space and a causal commutator --- at the level of the two-point function only, since a four-point reflection form on the original composite comes out negative. The positive realization is therefore not a sector of the fermion model.

Abstract: We present topological field theories propagating biform singleton fields as edge modes. In even spacetime dimensions $D=2p$, these are irreducible representations of the conformal algebra $\mathfrak{so}(2,2p)$ with two-column Young diagrams of height $p$. Their duality properties depend on the parity of $p$: They propagate chiral degrees of freedom for odd $p$, and exhibit electric--magnetic duality for even $p$. We are able to construct the topological actions by realising the hidden conformal symmetry inherent to singletons geometrically in terms of an auxiliary bulk $\text{AdS}_{2p+1}$ geometry. Using these as a starting point, we are able to apply a recently established covariant boundary reduction procedure to yield novel action formulations with manifest Lorentz covariance. We also show how, breaking manifest Lorentz covariance, we recover known formulations of biform singletons.

Abstract: We apply the functional renormalization group to the worldsheet theory of the bosonic string. Imposing the Weyl anomaly cancellation as a condition on the local renormalization group flow, we derive flow equations for the background couplings and the corresponding effective spacetime equations of motion, first in a two-derivative truncation, on a general curved background. Within this truncation, we obtain Tseytlin's spacetime effective action, and we use it to study solutions to all orders in $α'$, including black holes. TL;DR: 本文构建了反德西特（AdS）和德西特（dS）Jackiw-Teitelboim（JT）引力中固定拓扑闭宇宙的希尔伯特空间，通过引力路径积分，并系统处理了量子化中的诸多技术难点，最终给出了物理态内积与概率诠释的严格框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力量子化面临诸多微妙问题，如lapse函数积分路径选择、路径积分测度、算符排序、规范群全局结构、约束条件实施及初末时刻边界规范对称性等；需建立统一形式体系以赋予宇宙波函数明确的概率诠释。

Method: 采用路径积分方法构造固定拓扑闭宇宙的希尔伯特空间；结合BFV-BRST哈密顿形式，系统处理lapse轮廓、测度、约束实施与初末时刻规范固定；精确计算内积并导出恒等式分解；区分协不变与不变希尔伯特空间。

Result: 得到了JT引力中固定拓扑与lapse轮廓下的精确内积与恒等式分解；明确了固定几何数据的引力波函数作为协不变态的严格含义及其积分测度；指出圆柱几何足以刻画所有微扰态（含Hartle-Hawking无边界态）的引力内积。

Conclusion: 该形式体系为AdS/dS JT引力中闭宇宙的量子化提供了自洽框架，厘清了波函数的概率诠释基础，且希尔伯特空间全局结构依赖于大爆炸/大挤压附近的处理方式，不同于纯BF理论。

Abstract: We construct the Hilbert space of a closed universe of fixed topology in anti-de Sitter (AdS) and de Sitter (dS) Jackiw-Teitelboim (JT) gravity from the gravitational path integral. Quantizing gravity involves several subtleties, including the choice of lapse contour, path-integral measure, operator ordering, global structure of the gauge group, the implementation of gravitational constraints and the gauge symmetries at initial and final timeslices. We develop a formalism that addresses these issues and determines the inner product of physical states, providing a framework to assign probabilities to cosmological wavefunctions. For a fixed topology and a lapse contour, we compute the inner product exactly and obtain the corresponding resolution of the identity in JT gravity. We demonstrate that the co-invariant and invariant Hilbert spaces in refined algebraic quantization arise from particular gauge fixing at the initial and final timeslices. In particular, the formalism provides precise meaning for a gravitational wavefunction with fixed geometric data as a co-invariant state, including its integral measure. These constructions can be formulated within the Hamiltonian BFV-BRST formalism. We also discuss the global structure of the Hilbert space, which differs from that of BF theory and depends on the prescription adopted near the big bang/crunch. Our results imply that the cylinder geometry captures the gravitational inner product for all perturbative states, including the Hartle-Hawking no-boundary state.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用数值积分Virasoro共形块并结合DOZZ结构常数的方法，计算树级（最高6点）和一环（最高3点）散射与热振幅，以及2亏格热自由能。

Result: 所得散射振幅、热振幅及 genus-two 热自由能均与c=1矩阵模型的预言完全吻合。

Conclusion: 世界面计算方法在c=1弦理论中是自洽且可靠的，进一步支持了弦理论与矩阵模型之间的对偶性。

Abstract: We revisit the $S$-matrix, the thermal $S$-matrix and the thermal free energy of $c=1$ string theory from the worldsheet perspective. By numerically integrating Virasoro conformal blocks with DOZZ structure constants, we compute the scattering amplitudes and thermal amplitudes up to 6-point at tree level and up to 3-point at one loop, in addition to the genus two thermal free energy. All results are in agreement with the predicted answers from the $c=1$ matrix model.

</details>


### [98] [Compact holography via polarized IKKT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03807)
*Arsam Najafian,Mark Van Raamsdonk,Alejandro Vilar López*

TL;DR: 本文建立了极化IKKT矩阵模型与IIB型超引力理论之间的直接对应关系，通过电势映射将矩阵模型鞍点精确转化为超引力鞍点，并验证了二者在作用量和保护观测量上的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立极化IKKT矩阵模型与IIB型超弦/超引力理论之间的严格对偶关系，特别是从有限N矩阵模型出发导出超引力解。

Method: 利用矩阵模型的resolvent构造全纯函数，再映射为2D库仑势、4D库仑势，最终得到超引力场；结合电动力学图像、局域化变形、鞍点分析及大N极限分析。

Result: 实现了矩阵模型鞍点到超引力鞍点的逐级映射；证明了二者在壳作用量和保护观测量完全匹配；发现超引力解在弦帧下具有紧致非奇异度规，爱因斯坦帧渐近区被点状D-瞬子源取代；提出可通过限制矩阵积分区域至对应鞍点吸引域来提取特定引力物理；识别出若干大N退耦极限，对应高维场论的引力对偶。

Conclusion: 极化IKKT矩阵模型不仅在大N极限下有效描述IIB超引力，其有限N结构已蕴含完整超引力几何与物理信息，为矩阵理论作为量子引力基本框架提供了关键支持。

Abstract: The polarized IKKT matrix model is a $U(N)$ supersymmetric matrix integral proposed to be dual to an $N$ D-instanton sector of Euclidean type IIB string theory. We show a direct path from matrix model saddles to supergravity saddles: matrix model resolvent $\to$ holomorphic function $\to$ 2D Coulomb potential $\to$ 4D Coulomb potential $\to$ supergravity fields, where the last step was previously described. Here, the 2D electrostatics picture is an exact finite $N$ reformulation of a localization deformation of the matrix model that gives a constrained gas of dipoles in a background field. We show a matching between supergravity and matrix model on-shell actions and protected observables for all saddles. We show that the supergravity saddles have a compact and non-singular metric in string frame with the Einstein frame asymptotic region replaced by a pointlike D-instanton source. We provide evidence that the physics of particular gravity saddles can be obtained through restricting the domain of the matrix model integral to a basin associated with the corresponding matrix model saddle. We describe certain large $N$ decoupling limits where families of the saddles decouple from the rest to give the gravity duals of certain higher-dimensional field theories.

</details>


### [99] [Resonant electroweak clouds around Schwarzschild black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03809)
*Carlos Herdeiro,Eugen Radu*

TL;DR: 本文在Weinberg-Salam理论框架下，构造了围绕Schwarzschild黑洞的非线性弱电云结构，其由谐波时变带电规范场支撑，能量动量张量静态；解需满足视界正则性所导出的共振条件，并表现出质量与电荷间隙、存在 horizon 尺寸上限等新特性；发现了磁/电型、球对称/偶极型等多种构型，包括一种新型偶极弱电球，且在固定电荷下可能比球形更稳定；结果在探针极限及偏离实测弱电耦合参数下获得，为非阿贝尔规范-希格斯理论中非线性共振矢量毛发提供了新实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索黑洞是否存在除质量、角动量、电荷外的其他‘毛发’（即外部可观测的额外自由度），特别是在非阿贝尔规范-希格斯理论（如Weinberg-Salam电弱理论）中，构建具有物理意义的非线性稳定‘矢量毛发’解。

Method: 在bosonic Weinberg-Salam理论中，采用探针极限近似，求解含时但能量动量张量静态的非线性规范-希格斯场方程；施加视界正则性边界条件，导出频率与视界静电势之间的共振关系；数值构造多种对称性（球对称、偶极）的磁/电型弱电云及弱电球解。

Result: 发现一类由谐波时变规范场支撑的非线性弱电云，其能量动量张量静态；存在质量与电荷间隙；仅当黑洞视界半径小于某一极大值时存在；获得磁型、电型、球对称和偶极型解；发现新型偶极弱电球，在固定电荷下能量可低于球形弱电球；所有解在探针极限及非实测耦合下成立。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在电弱尺度的非阿贝尔规范-希格斯理论中实现了非线性共振矢量毛发，揭示了黑洞与标准模型标量-规范场之间新颖的非线性束缚态行为，拓展了‘无毛定理’在现实粒子物理模型中的适用边界。

Abstract: We construct nonlinear electroweak clouds around Schwarzschild black holes in the bosonic Weinberg-Salam theory. These configurations are supported by harmonically time-dependent charged gauge fields, while their energy-momentum tensor remains static. Regularity at the horizon imposes a resonance condition relating the field frequency to the horizon electrostatic potential. Unlike linear clouds, these solutions exhibit a finite mass and electric charge gap, and exist only up to a maximal horizon size. We obtain both magnetic and electric clouds, including spherically symmetric and dipolar configurations, which reduce to electroweak balls in the vanishing-horizon limit. We also report a new family of dipolar electroweak balls, which can be energetically favoured over their spherical counterparts at fixed electric charge. Our results, obtained in the probe limit for electroweak couplings away from their measured values, establish a new realization of nonlinear resonant vector hair in a non-Abelian gauge-Higgs theory.

</details>

*Rolando Ramirez Camasca,Xiang Li,Ting-Chun Lin,John McGreevy*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从正则化共形场论基态重构整体全局共形代数的新方法，该方法优于基于哈密顿量密度的传统构造，在模糊球正则化下表现更优、模型无关、无需额外调参且随系统尺寸增大而改进；同时成功识别了部分共形原初态，并为短程纠缠哈密顿量修正模型提供了新证据并实现系统性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重建共形代数以识别共形原初态，并验证和改进短距离下纠缠哈密顿量的修正模型。

Method: 从同一球面哈密顿量的基态出发，利用受控的共形场论基态重构整个全局共形代数，采用模糊球正则化进行检验。

Result: 所提方法优于基于哈密顿量密度的共形生成元构造，具有模型无关性、无需调参、随系统尺寸增大而精度提升等优势；成功识别部分共形原初态；为先前提出的短距纠缠哈密顿量修正模型提供了进一步支持，并实现了系统性改进。

Conclusion: 基于基态重构共形代数是一种更稳健、普适且可扩展的方法，不仅有助于共形对称性分析与原初态识别，也为纠缠哈密顿量的精确建模提供了新路径。

Abstract: Recently, we found a method to reconstruct the dilatation operator (sphere Hamiltonian) from a regulated conformal field theory groundstate. In this paper, we explore methods to construct the whole global conformal algebra, and test our construction using fuzzy sphere regularization. Starting from a groundstate of the same Hamiltonian, these methods perform better than constructions of the conformal generators based on a Hamiltonian density, are model-independent, require no additional tuning, and improve with system size. One purpose of reconstructing the conformal algebra is to identify conformal primary states, which we do with some success. We also find further evidence supporting the model of the short-distance corrections to the entanglement Hamiltonian proposed earlier and use it to make systematic improvements.

</details>


### [101] [A lattice construction of the Kormos-Runkel-Watts defect flows in minimal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03824)
*Junchen Rong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了最小模型M(p,p+1)中(2,1)Verlinde缺陷在自旋缺陷场φ_{(1,3)}⊗φ̄_{(3,3)}驱动下的重整化群流，发现其流向新的共形界面，并通过数值方法（精确对角化和DMRG）验证了该流在p=4和p=5时的不同终点；同时，通过规范η操作将所得界面映射到三态Potts模型界面，并利用环模型连续追踪C_p族界面行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解拓扑缺陷在弱相关扰动下的RG流行为及其最终稳定的共形界面性质，特别是探索其在不同p值下的连续变化及与其它统计模型（如Potts模型）的联系。

Method: 采用稀疏RSOS晶格实现、精确对角化（ED）和密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）进行数值研究；结合Verlinde线对称性分析、规范变换（gauging）以及环模型公式化方法。

Result: 在p=4时流至可逆线η=D_{(3,1)}；在p=5时流至非平凡共形界面C_5，其边界熵g=1.503(2)，透射系数T=0.665(10)，并给出其能谱、主无关算子维度和晶格β函数；C_p族界面随p从5降至4平滑过渡至η。

Conclusion: 该缺陷流具有由(r,1)Verlinde线保护的对称性，支持稀疏RSOS实现；所得C_5界面经η规范后对应于受Fibonacci线和电荷共轭约束的稳定三态Potts界面；C_p界面族在4≤p≤5区间内连续演化，表明该类共形界面具有普适性和结构性稳定性。

Abstract: Kormos, Runkel, and Watts (KRW) showed that a topological defect of a diagonal minimal model $M(p,p+1)$, perturbed by a weakly relevant defect field, flows to a new conformal interface. We study the flow of the $(2,1)$ Verlinde defect $D_{(2,1)}$ driven by the spinful defect field $φ_{(1,3)}\otimes\barφ_{(3,3)}$ on the lattice. The flow preserves the $(r,1)$ Verlinde lines. This preserved symmetry makes a dilute RSOS realisation possible. We study this flow numerically using both exact diagonalization and density matrix renormalization group (DMRG). At $p=4$, the flow ends on the invertible line $η=D_{(3,1)}$. At $p=5$ it ends on a non-trivial conformal interface $C_5$ with $g=1.503(2)$ and transmission coefficient $T=0.665(10)$. We report its spectrum, leading irrelevant dimension, and lattice $β$-function. Gauging $η$ maps $C_5$ to an interface of the three-state Potts model, which breaks $\mathbb{Z}_3$ but is a stable interface once the Fibonacci line $τ_{\mathrm{Fib}}$ and charge conjugation are imposed. A loop-model formulation allows us to follow the family $C_p$ at continuous $4\le p\le5$: it moves smoothly from $C_5$ to $η$ as we lower $p$.

</details>


### [102] [From off-shell strings to entropy flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03827)
*Houwen Wu,Shuxuan Ying*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为全息熵的比特线（bit threads）描述提供了弦论基础，通过构建动力学探针弦（probe-string）来实现最大熵流，并推导出弦帧下的局部容量约束和熵密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特线虽能表达全息熵，但未指明其物理载体；本文旨在从弦论出发，为比特线的局部容量约束和最大流提供物理实现。

Method: 利用球面离壳作用量的圆锥变分确定弦帧熵密度；结合引力副本构造与加权流-割对偶导出弦帧范数约束；构造BTZ黑洞中正则稳态F1弦解（空间部分为对数螺旋），并推广至含混合NS-NS与RR通量的背景。

Result: 成功构建了稳态探针弦作为最大熵流的物理实现，其投影弦荷密度守恒且模长恒定，通量精确等于BTZ熵；不同通量比例下仍复现相同主导熵，验证了弦论中范数约束与最大流的一致性。

Conclusion: 比特线所依赖的局部容量约束及其最大流表征均可由弦论自然导出，稳态弦提供了全息熵流的物理通道，弥合了几何描述与物理载体之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Bit threads express holographic entropy as the maximal flux of a divergenceless vector field subject to a local capacity bound, but do not identify a physical carrier of that flux. In our previous work, we identified the projected string charge density with a bit-thread flow, reproduced the entropies of BTZ and ten-dimensional three-charge black holes, and showed that the maximal entropy flux is preserved under S- and T-dualities. However, the norm bound was imported from the bit-thread prescription, while the string-current configurations were prescribed along known bit-thread lines. In this paper, we give a stringy foundation for the local capacity and construct dynamical probe-string representatives of maximal flow. At genus zero and two-derivative order, the conical variation of the target-space action derived from the off-shell sphere fixes the string-frame entropy density. The gravitational replica construction and weighted flow-cut duality then yield the string-frame norm bound. We next find regular stationary F1 solutions in an NS-NS supported BTZ black hole whose spatial parts are logarithmic spirals. A uniform ensemble produces a conserved, constant-norm projected string charge density and realizes a maximal flow whose flux equals the BTZ entropy. We finally extend the construction to BTZ backgrounds with mixed NS-NS and RR flux. Varying the flux fraction changes the stationary F1 configurations at fixed metric and dilaton, while the corresponding conserved flows reproduce the same leading BTZ entropy. Thus, both the norm bound and its physical maximal-flow representative arise from string theory, with stationary strings providing physical channels for the entropic flux represented geometrically by bit threads.

</details>


### [103] [Exotic Pauli spinors and magnetic flux quantization in a superconducting ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03877)
*L. Ibrain Cohen Iskandar,J. M. Hoff da Silva,R. T. Cavalcanti*
