TL;DR: 本文发展了奇异Pauli方程的形式理论，建立了标准与奇异Pauli旋量之间的有效对称性，用于在非相对论框架下描述超导环中的磁通量子化；该对称性在运动方程、拉格朗日量和流密度层面严格成立，且无需截断奇异项或限制拓扑相的时间依赖性；作者还显式构造了实现该对称性的复合物质-规范幺正算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在非相对论框架下统一描述超导环中磁通量子化现象，需建立标准与奇异自旋自由度之间的严格对称性，并确保其在量子层面的可实现性。

Method: 基于协变奇异Dirac方程确定时空拓扑项符号，推导奇异Pauli方程；系统比较标准与奇异情形下的运动方程、拉格朗日量及电流；导出磁通量子化条件，并构造作用于希尔伯特空间的复合物质-规范幺正算符。

Result: 证实了标准与奇异Pauli旋量之间存在严格（无近似、无附加条件）的对称性，覆盖经典到量子全层次；显式给出实现该对称性的幺正算符，其中规范部分需常规重整化处理。

Conclusion: 奇异Pauli形式论不仅自洽，而且与标准描述具有深层对称结构，为理解拓扑效应与超导量子现象提供了新的非相对论视角与工具。

Abstract: We develop the formalism of the exotic Pauli equation and establish an effective symmetry between standard and exotic Pauli spinors to describe magnetic flux quantization in a superconducting ring within the nonrelativistic framework. We show that, once the signs of the spatial and temporal topological terms are fixed consistently by the covariant exotic Dirac equation, this symmetry is exact at the level of the equations of motion, Lagrangians and currents, with no truncation of the exotic term and no condition on the time dependence of the topological phase. Upon deriving the flux quantization condition, we explicitly construct the composite matter-gauge unitary operator acting on the Hilbert space, which implements the symmetry at the quantum level (the gauge sector requiring the usual regularization).

</details>


### [104] [Hamiltonian of open string field theory for time-dependent solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03941)
*Vinícius Bernardes,Riccardo Poletti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用L∞理论中守恒荷的新表达式，给出了Witten玻色开弦场论哈密顿量的显式形式，并在Erler-Maccaferri解析解上进行计算，精确复现了Sen关于滚动快子能量的边界态结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确计算弦场论中特定背景（如Dp-膜和滚动快子）的能量，需建立严格且可计算的哈密顿量表达式。

Method: 基于L∞代数框架下的守恒荷公式，推导Witten开弦场论的哈密顿量；将其应用于Erler-Maccaferri解析解及滚动快子解，并与边界态方法对比。

Result: 得到了Witten开弦场论哈密顿量的显式表达式；在Erler-Maccaferri解上成功计算Dp-膜能量；滚动快子能量结果与Sen的边界态计算完全一致。

Conclusion: 证实了L∞理论中守恒荷公式在弦场论能量计算中的有效性，强化了解析解与BCFT物理图像之间的自洽性。

Abstract: Using the recently introduced expression for conserved charges of $L_\infty$ theories, we give an explicit form for the Hamiltonian of Witten's bosonic open string field theory. We evaluate it on the Erler-Maccaferri analytic solution, describing any D$p$-brane configuration associated to a BCFT. We use the expression to compute the energy of the rolling tachyon solution and find exact agreement with the boundary state result by Sen.

</details>


### [105] [Obstruction to local entropy laws on arbitrary black-hole cuts in five-dimensional Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03970)
*Cendikiawan Suryaatmadja,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维爱因斯坦-高斯-博内引力中事件视界任意截面上的局域熵律，证明在一定光滑性假设下，不存在既能复现稳态黑洞熵又满足任意有序截面对之间非减性的协变局域熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义相对论高阶修正理论（如Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力）中是否存在满足热力学第二定律的协变局域黑洞熵定义。

Method: 基于局域熵密度在度规与截面喷射空间上的光滑性假设，构造真空黑洞受引力波扰动的反例，并利用剪切导致截面内曲率依赖于弯曲程度这一几何事实进行分析。

Result: 证明在五维Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力中，不存在满足稳态熵匹配和普遍熵增的协变局域熵；该障碍出现在引力波振幅二阶、光滑视界上，无需并合或拓扑改变。

Conclusion: 局域熵第二定律在高阶引力理论中不成立，表明黑洞热力学的局域化存在根本性困难，需重新审视熵的几何定义或考虑非局域结构。

Abstract: We study local entropy laws on arbitrary cuts of event horizons in five-dimensional Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet gravity, treated as an exact theory. No covariant local entropy can both reproduce the stationary black-hole entropy and be nondecreasing between every ordered pair of cuts, provided its density is smooth on an invariant open set of off-shell metric and cut jets containing a flat planar cut and every cut used in the argument. No global bound on derivative order is assumed. The counterexamples to the second law are vacuum black holes perturbed by a gravitational wave, with a strongly hyperbolic formulation near the relevant horizon interval and its stationary future. Shear makes the intrinsic curvature of a cut depend on its bending. The stationary entropy fixes the coefficient of this curvature term, and local corrections cannot cancel its response to cut bending. The obstruction appears at second order in wave amplitude on a smooth horizon, without a merger or topology change.

</details>


### [106] [Hermitian matrix-valued nonlocal deformation of the SU($N$) Thirring model at large $N$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03973)
*J. R. Nascimento,P. J. Porfírio,C. Furtado*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维SU(N) Thirring模型在1/N展开领头阶下的厄米矩阵值非局域变形，通过直接在原始作用量上施加f(∂̸)†=f(∂̸)约束，构造并分析了质量偶对的大N有效势及其径向核、弱耦合维度变换能标和Lambert-W零点，并指出所得能隙与谱零点属于所定义的平均场泛函，需进一步推导才能获得未配对理论中的物理能隙与费米子极点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为探索非局域性对强耦合量子场论（特别是Thirring模型）的影响，以及理解非局域结构下大N极限中能隙生成与谱性质的机制。

Method: 在1/N展开领头阶下，对二维SU(N) Thirring模型引入满足厄米性条件f(∂̸)†=f(∂̸)的非局域形变；选取指数型非局域核f(∂̸)=exp[−i∂̸/Λ]；构造并分析偶对（even-in-mass）大N有效势，计算其径向核、维度转移动能标及Lambert-W形式的零点；考察径向能隙积分的截断极限收敛性。

Result: 得到了偶对大N有效势的径向核表达式；确定了弱耦合下的维度转移动能标；导出了形式化的Lambert-W零点；发现径向能隙积分在无穷截断下条件收敛；指出该有效势基于配对处方，不同于原始厄米作用量的未配对行列式。

Conclusion: 所得能隙与谱零点反映的是所明确定义的配对平均场泛函的性质，而非原始未配对理论的物理可观测量；要获得真实的物理质量能隙和费米子极点，尚需从原始未配对理论出发进行进一步推导。

Abstract: We investigate a Hermitian matrix-valued nonlocal deformation of the two-dimensional $SU(N)$ Thirring model at leading order in $1/N$. We impose $f(\slashed{\partial})^\ddagger=f(\slashed{\partial})$ directly on the original action; for real nonlocality scale the admissible exponential choice is $f(\slashed{\partial})=\exp[-i\slashed{\partial}/Λ]$. We construct and analyze the even-in-mass (paired) large-$N$ effective potential and derive its radial kernel, weak-coupling dimensional-transmutation scale, and formal Lambert-$W$ zeros. Its radial gap integral possesses a conditionally convergent infinite-cutoff limit. The pairing prescription, however, differs from the unpaired determinant of the original Hermitian action, so the reported gap and spectral zeros must be understood as properties of this explicitly defined mean-field functional. We identify the further derivation needed to obtain physical mass gaps and fermion poles from the unpaired theory.

</details>


### [107] [A Ghost-Free dS/AdS Correspondence in $\mathcal{N}=1$ Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03977)
*Omer Guleryuz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在超对称背景下实现de Sitter与anti-de Sitter真空之间点对点映射的新机制，该机制基于超共形Kähler锥内的Z2反射，保持理论健康（无鬼），并为弦紧化、dS沼泽地猜想及慢滚暴胀提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统符号翻转操作在超对称情形下导致宇宙学侧出现鬼场的问题，建立健康、可实现的dS/AdS对应关系。

Method: 利用超共形框架下的Kähler锥结构，通过对nilpotent goldstino Kähler度量关于V=0平直极限的Z2反射，实现标量势V↔−V的点对点映射，同时保持可见Kähler度量、超势和幺正规范费米子拉氏量不变。

Result: 构建了两个普通、无鬼的N=1四维超引力理论之间的对应；导出配对真空满足H=1/L、m²_dS=−m²_AdS等精确字典关系；稳定性窗口由Breitenlohner–Freedman界限定为0≤m²_dS≤9/4 H²；将慢滚暴胀映射为满足全息c定理的健康AdS域壁。

Conclusion: 该对应是超对称、无鬼且自洽的，为理解dS真空的量子引力相容性、弦论中反D3膜真空的分类以及暴胀与全息原理的联系提供了新框架。

Abstract: Reversing the sign of the scalar potential exchanges de Sitter and anti-de Sitter vacua and, through the domain-wall/cosmology correspondence, maps flat domain walls onto flat FLRW cosmologies, but its supersymmetric version has ghosts on the cosmological side. We show that this pointwise correspondence is realized between two ordinary, ghost-free four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ supergravities with spontaneously broken supersymmetry. Within a single superconformal Kähler cone, a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ reflection of the nilpotent goldstino Kähler metric through the flat limit $V=0$ reverses $V\leftrightarrow-V$ pointwise while preserving the visible Kähler metric, the superpotential, and the unitary-gauge fermion Lagrangian. The paired vacua obey a sharp dictionary with $H=1/L$, $m^2_{\rm dS}=-m^2_{\rm AdS}$, equal conformal weights, and $m_{3/2}>H$, and are simultaneously stable precisely within the window $0\le m^2_{\rm dS}\le\frac94H^2$ set by the Breitenlohner--Freedman bound. Applied to string compactifications and cosmology, this correspondence pairs over-uplifted with under-uplifted anti-D3-brane vacua, confines the de Sitter swampland conjecture to the goldstino (anti-brane) sector, and maps slow-roll inflation onto a healthy AdS domain wall obeying the holographic $c$-theorem.

</details>


### [108] [Negative Junction Logarithms in Scalar Multipartite Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04220)
*Chen-Te Ma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了2+1维自由实相对论性标量真空中三区域结的真正三方Rényi多熵，发现对所有整数n≥3及非退化扇形角，其对数项严格为负；通过副本减法精确消除单界面相关环类，剩余环具有正的、尺度无关的权重，导致符号由负Rényi因子决定的对数项；n=2时选择器为空；采用平直片Friedrichs约定在非流形副本顶点固定标量算符，导出角谱公式以计算系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维量子场论中多区域纠缠结构，特别是三区域结在低维（2+1维）自由标量真空中的真正三方纠缠行为及其Rényi熵的普适对数贡献。

Method: 采用副本方法（replica method），结合 replica subtraction 技术消除单界面环类；利用 flat-sheet Friedrichs prescription 处理非流形副本顶点处的标量算符边界条件；推导并分析角谱形式的收敛表达式以计算对数项系数。

Result: 证明对任意整数n≥3及所有非退化扇形角，三区域结的真正三方Rényi多熵存在严格负的对数发散项；该结果不依赖于尺度，且n=2时无此贡献（selector为空）。

Conclusion: 三区域几何构型在2+1维自由标量理论中诱导出普适、负定的三方纠缠对数项，揭示了多体纠缠结构与时空几何奇点之间的深刻联系，并为高阶Rényi熵的几何诠释提供了新证据。

Abstract: A three-region junction in a free real relativistic scalar vacuum in $2+1$ dimensions yields a strictly negative logarithmic contribution to genuine tripartite Rényi multi-entropy for every integer $n\geq3$ and all nondegenerate sector angles. The replica subtraction exactly removes loop classes associated with a single interface. Surviving loops have positive, scale-independent weight, yielding a logarithm whose sign is fixed by the negative Rényi prefactor. The selector is empty at $n=2$. The flat-sheet Friedrichs prescription fixes the scalar operator at the nonmanifold replica apex. It yields a convergent angular spectral formula for the coefficient.

</details>


### [109] [On the universality of entanglement entropy in quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04238)
*Orr Sela*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了量子场论中纠缠熵定义中正则化边界条件的选择对熵的普适性的影响，通过三维Chern-Simons理论和二维共形场论为例，证明在满足一定假设下，不同可导致幺正映射的边界条件仅影响非普适项，而普适项保持不变；非幺正映射则可能改变普适项，并进一步讨论了一般理论中普适性的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论中纠缠熵的标准定义依赖于对纠缠面的正则化及希尔伯特空间的分解，而这涉及正则化面上边界条件的选择；目前尚不清楚：（1）何种条件下存在能导出幺正映射的边界条件（这是定义良好纠缠熵的必要条件）；（2）若存在多种此类选择，它们是否给出相同的（至少是普适部分的）熵。

Method: 以拓扑场论（特别是三维Chern-Simons理论）为具体模型进行分析，结合幺正性要求与熵的结构分解；推广至二维共形场论，并提出一般理论中熵普适性的判据。

Result: 在指定假设下，不同能实现幺正映射的边界条件仅改变纠缠熵的非普适项，其普适项保持不变；而对应非幺正映射的边界条件可改变普适项；该结论亦适用于二维CFT，并给出了一般理论中熵普适性的条件。

Conclusion: 纠缠熵的普适部分本质上由理论的拓扑/共形性质决定，只要边界条件保证幺正性，其选择不影响普适项；因此，普适纠缠熵具有内在稳健性；该结果为纠缠熵作为普适物理量的可靠性提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: The standard definition of entanglement entropy in quantum field theory requires regularizing the entangling surface and factorizing the Hilbert space, which in turn involves a choice of boundary conditions at the regularized surface. This raises two natural questions which are open at present. The first is under what conditions there is a choice of boundary conditions that leads to a unitary map between the original Hilbert space and the factorized one, which is a necessary condition for a well-defined entanglement entropy. The second concerns the situation where there are multiple such choices, in which case the question is whether they all yield the same entropy, or at least the same terms usually referred to as universal. Focusing on topological theories and using three-dimensional Chern-Simons theory as a concrete illustration, we show under specified assumptions that different such choices affect only the non-universal terms in the entropy, such that the remaining terms are universal in this sense as well, while we explicitly demonstrate that boundary conditions corresponding to non-unitary maps can change these terms. We then explain how a similar argument applies to two-dimensional conformal field theories and discuss a condition for universality in general theories.

</details>


### [110] [Gauge-invariant thermodynamic description of rotating black strings in 5D minimal supergravity via Kaluza-Klein reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04373)
*Yang Yu,Peng-Ju Hu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Kaluza-Klein约化，构建了五维极小超引力中旋转黑弦的规范不变热力学理论，并计算了四阶导数修正，结果与四维Kerr-Newman黑洞一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立五维旋转黑弦在超引力框架下的规范不变热力学描述，并验证高阶修正的一致性。

Method: 采用Kaluza-Klein约化处理五维最小超引力中的黑弦系统，完整约化体作用量与GHY边界项（含Chern-Simons诱导面项），得到四维N=2超引力；使用Hamilton形式建立欧氏壳上作用量与Gibbs自由能的量子统计关系；利用Reall-Santos方法计算四阶导数修正。

Result: 获得四维N=2超引力（预势P=1/(3√3)·X₁³/X₀）中旋转磁性黑弦的热力学及其四阶导数修正，结果与四维Kerr-Newman黑洞的已有结果精确一致。

Conclusion: 五维黑弦经KK约化后热力学行为与四维Kerr-Newman黑洞统一，验证了不同维度与构型下高阶修正的普适性及方法的可靠性。

Abstract: We develop a gauge-invariant formulation of the thermodynamics of rotating black strings in five-dimensional minimal supergravity through Kaluza-Klein reduction. At the two-derivative level, we consistently reduce the bulk action and the Gibbons-Hawking-York boundary term, carefully accounting for the surface contributions induced by the Chern-Simons coupling. The resulting four-dimensional theory is $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity coupled to a single vector multiplet, with prepotential $P=\frac{1}{3\sqrt{3}}\frac{X_1^3}{X_0}$. Employing the Hamiltonian formalism, we establish the quantum statistical relation between the Euclidean on-shell action and the Gibbs free energy. We further compute the leading four-derivative corrections to the thermodynamics of a rotating magnetic black string using the Reall-Santos method. The resulting corrections are in precise agreement with previous results obtained directly for the Kerr-Newman black hole in four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity.

</details>


### [111] [Circuit Complexity of Scalar and Tensor Perturbations in Luminal Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04441)
*Tao Li,Hai-Bing Fu,Lei-Hua Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-高斯-博内（EGB）暴胀模型中原始标量与张量扰动的量子电路复杂度，发现尽管张量传播速度为光速，高斯-博内耦合仍通过泵场动态影响扰动演化；标量与张量的挤压振幅出现明显分离，导致其波函数复杂度呈现相反趋势，揭示二者在相同修正暴胀背景中仍携带不同信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究修正引力理论（如EGB引力）下暴胀宇宙学中量子扰动的复杂度特征，以区分标量与张量模式对新物理的响应，尤其当张量速度被钉扎为光速时是否仍能提取独立信息。

Method: 从二次扰动作用量出发构建双模哈密顿量，推导挤压振幅与角度的演化方程，数值追踪从亚视界到超视界阶段的挤压动力学，并计算对应双模压缩态的Nielsen波函数复杂度。

Result: 高斯-博内修正导致标量与张量挤压振幅显著分离，而挤压角度近似相位锁定；电路复杂度主要由振幅演化主导；正GB耦合增强标量复杂度但轻微抑制张量复杂度。

Conclusion: 即使张量传播为光速，标量与张量高斯态仍通过其复杂度演化保留对同一修正暴胀背景的不同响应，表明量子复杂度可作为探测早期宇宙新物理的独立观测量。

Abstract: We investigate the circuit complexity of primordial scalar and tensor perturbations in the luminal branch of Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet inflation. Starting from the quadratic perturbation actions, we construct the two-mode Hamiltonians and derive the evolution equations for the squeezing amplitudes and angles. Although the tensor speed is fixed to unity, the Gauss--Bonnet (GB) coupling remains dynamical through the scalar and tensor pump fields. We track the squeezing dynamics from the subhorizon to the superhorizon regime and evaluate the Nielsen wave-function complexity of the resulting two-mode squeezed states. The GB correction produces a clear separation in the squeezing amplitudes, while the unwrapped squeezing angles remain nearly phase locked. Consequently, the circuit complexity is controlled mainly by the amplitude evolution. A positive coupling enhances the scalar complexity but slightly suppresses the tensor complexity for the benchmarks considered. These opposite trends show that scalar and tensor Gaussian states retain distinct information about the same modified inflationary background even when tensor propagation is luminal.

</details>


### [112] [Regge bounds and eikonal scaling in a four-dimensional scalar EFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04505)
*Ji Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了无质量平移对称标量场中高维算符对色散关系和正定性边界的影响，通过分析树图、单圈泡图和三圈香蕉图在大s极限下的增长行为，并结合分波展开与eikonal相位方法，利用前向极限下的吸收部分约束耦合常数g4和g6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统正定性边界依赖于二重减色散关系，要求振幅增长慢于s²；但含高维导数算符的理论可能破坏该假设，需重新推导适用的色散关系并建立新的约束方法。

Method: 推导适用于零能支点的色散关系；分析固定t<0下树图、单圈泡图和三圈香蕉图的大s行为；采用分波展开，低自旋由幺正性逐道限制，高自旋用交叉道对数项的eikonal相位结合驻相法估算；在前向极限下利用一阶和三阶吸收部分施加约束。

Result: 得到了适用于含g4(∂φ)⁴/Λ⁴和g6(∂φ)⁶/Λ⁸项理论的修正色散关系；明确了各阶图在大s下的增长特性；给出了基于吸收部分对g4和g6的有效约束。

Conclusion: 标准正定性边界需推广以涵盖零能支点情形；高维导数算符可通过前向吸收部分与eikonal方法进行有效约束，为EFT有效性提供新判据。

Abstract: Positivity bounds rest on twice-subtracted dispersion relations, which assume that the amplitude grows more slowly than $s^2$. For the massless shift-symmetric scalar with $g_4(\partialφ)^4/Λ^4$ and $g_6(\partialφ)^6/Λ^8$, we derive the dispersion relation, whose cuts start at zero energy, and study the large-$s$ growth at fixed $t<0$ of the tree, the one-loop bubble and the three-loop banana. The partial-wave sum is split at a transition spin: low spins are bounded by unitarity, channel by channel, and high spins are evaluated with the eikonal phase of the crossed-channel logarithms by stationary phase. The same construction is applied in the forward limit required by the dispersion relation, and we use the one- and three-loop absorptive parts below the cutoff to constrain $g_4$ and $g_6$.

</details>


### [113] [Modular properties of correlators in generalised Gibbs ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04636)
*Tanmoy Sengupta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维共形场论广义吉布斯系综中环面关联函数的模性质，以自由费米子（含KdV荷）为例精确计算了两点半关联函数，并推导其关于化学势展开的模S变换；发现变换后关联函数可分解为有效电荷改变系综与局域“穿衣算符”作用于外场两部分，二者均由变形流的嵌套OPE确定；进而提出有理手性CFT中n点函数的类似变换猜想，并借助Zhu递归在模参数最低阶、全阶化学势下证明该猜想并确定穿衣算符，且验证至化学势三次方精度；成果将GGE配分函数的嵌套OPE模变换描述推广至局域关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义吉布斯系综（GGE）下共形场论关联函数的模对称性，拓展已知的GGE配分函数模变换结构到局域算符相关函数。

Method: 以自由费米子模型为具体例子，精确计算两点半关联函数并展开其模S-变换；引入嵌套算符乘积展开（nested OPE）描述变形流对系综和外场的影响；提出适用于有理手性CFT的n点函数模变换猜想；利用Zhu递归方法，在模参数低阶展开下严格证明猜想并确定 dressing operator，并验证至化学势三次方。

Result: 获得自由费米子GGE中两点半函数的模S变换的显式形式，揭示其因子化结构（有效电荷+局域穿衣算符）；提出并部分证明了有理手性CFT中n点函数的普适模变换猜想；确定了对应 dressing operator 的具体形式；将嵌套OPE框架从配分函数成功推广至局域关联函数。

Conclusion: GGE下CFT关联函数的模变换具有普适的嵌套OPE结构，由守恒流驱动，兼具系综重定义与场重归一化双重效应；该结构不仅适用于自由模型，也适用于更一般的有理手性CFT，标志着非平衡统计系综下共形对称性理解的重要进展。

Abstract: We study the modular properties of torus correlators in a generalised Gibbs ensemble (GGE) of a two-dimensional conformal field theory. For the free fermion with KdV charges, we compute the exact two-point function and derive its modular $S$-transformation as a formal power series in the chemical potential. The transformed correlator admits a factorised description: an effective charge deforms the ensemble, while a local dressing operator acts on the external fields. Both are determined by nested operator product expansions involving the deforming current. Motivated by this result, we conjecture an analogous transformation law for $n$-point functions in rational chiral conformal field theories. Using Zhu's recursion, we establish the conjecture at the lowest explicit power of the modular parameter to all orders in the chemical potential, thereby determining the dressing operator. We also verify the full conjectured transformation through cubic order in the chemical potential, retaining all explicit powers of the modular parameter. These results extend the nested-OPE description of modular transformations of GGE partition functions to local correlation functions.

</details>


### [114] [Scalar radiation from circular orbits in D3 brane spacetimes: a Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi-like approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04665)
*Donato Bini,Giorgio Di Russo,Veronica Fantini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了十维D3膜时空中的标量扰动，发展了一种适用于该情况的高维Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi形式论，并导出了Mathieu方程的源修正形式；进一步应用该理论计算了沿圆形‘赤道’测地线运动的标量电荷所辐射的标量波在无穷远和视界处的能量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维时空（特别是D3膜背景）中经典场扰动的动力学行为及其辐射特性，为探索引力波类现象、全息对偶及高维黑洞物理提供理论工具。

Method: 构建适用于十维D3膜时空的高维Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi形式论，将标量扰动问题约化为一个带源项的Mathieu型微分方程，并求解该方程以计算能量流（如Bolometric flux）在无穷远与事件视界处的分布。

Result: 成功导出描述标量扰动的修正Mathieu方程，并据此定量计算了作圆周运动的标量电荷在十维D3膜背景中向无穷远和视界辐射的能量损失率。

Conclusion: 标量扰动在D3膜时空中的传播可由修正Mathieu方程有效刻画，其辐射功率具有明确的几何与参数依赖性，该方法为高维弯曲时空中的波模分析提供了可推广的解析框架。

Abstract: We study scalar perturbations in a ten-dimensional D3 brane spacetime, developing a (higher-dimensional) Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi-like formalism adapted to the present case leading to a sourced modification of a Mathieu equation. As an application, we compute the energy loss both at infinity and at the horizon in terms of scalar waves due to a scalar charge moving along a circular "equatorial" geodesic.

</details>


### [115] [The Stringy Origin of Kerr Multipoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04702)
*Maurizio Firrotta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一类新的精确开闭弦解，能在大质量、大角动量极限下复现根-Kerr与Kerr多极矩，为弦态与旋转黑洞的对应提供经典数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为建立量子弦态与旋转黑洞之间的对应关系，需提供可明确识别的 classical string solutions 作为基准。

Method: 在平直时空下构造一类新的精确开弦和闭弦解，并分析其在大质量、大角动量极限下的多极结构。

Result: 所得解在每一固定多极阶上精确复现 root-Kerr 和 Kerr 多极矩。

Conclusion: 这些解提供了可用于识别对应量子弦态并检验其与旋转黑洞对偶性的显式经典数据。

Abstract: We construct a new family of exact open and closed bosonic string solutions in flat spacetime that reproduce the root-Kerr and Kerr multipoles at every fixed multipole order in the large-mass and large-angular-momentum limit. These solutions provide explicit classical data for identifying associated quantum string states and testing their correspondence with rotating black holes.

</details>


### [116] [Fortuitous BMN Operators from Multiple Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04722)
*Robert de Mello Koch,Sarthak Duary,Minkyoo Kim,Sinong Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统性构造N=4超杨-米尔斯理论BMN扇区中偶然上同调类的方法，通过低秩构建模块组合并修正有效字母，确保其满足对应低秩理论的超对称变换，并构造出规范不变、Q-闭的多项式算符；通过限制映射验证非正合性与偶然性，并以SU(2)核心组合构造SU(4)中的偶然类为例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解有限N下偶然上同调的结构，需避免繁琐的直接计算，建立系统性构造方法。

Method: 基于颜色空间任意划分，构造满足低秩理论超对称变换的修正有效字母，进而构建规范不变且Q-闭的多项式算符；引入限制映射以恢复构成成分并验证非正合性与偶然性。

Result: 成功系统性构造了BMN扇区中任意秩的偶然上同调类；以两个SU(2)偶然核心组合显式构造出SU(4)中的偶然类。

Conclusion: 该方法为有限N下偶然上同调的结构研究提供了普适、高效的系统性框架，避免了暴力计算。

Abstract: We develop a systematic construction of fortuitous cohomology classes in the BMN sector of $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory from multiple lower-rank building blocks. For arbitrary partitions of colour space, we construct corrected effective letters that obey the supersymmetry transformations of the corresponding lower-rank theories. These allow us to build gauge-invariant, $Q$-closed polynomial operators. A restriction map recovers the constituent cohomology classes, establishing non-exactness and fortuity without brute-force computations. As an explicit example, we combine two fortuitous $SU(2)$ cores to construct a fortuitous class in $SU(4)$. Our results provide a systematic approach to the structure of fortuitous cohomology at finite $N$.

</details>


### [117] [Crossover from Schwarzian dynamics to rotational soft modes in near-extremal Lovelock black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04726)
*Ankit Anand,Aditya Singh,Kimet Jusufi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究七维三阶Lovelock引力中近极值黑洞的软模计数及其对熵中log T项系数的影响，发现尽管模的多重性不变，但有效自由度随温度变化，导致log T系数从低温3/2跃升至高温9，且不达到高斯近似值19/2；同时计算了转动惯量K_J，并分析了Lovelock耦合对交叉尺度的影响，以及引入带电标量场后的近视界Schwinger阈值与BF界的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高阶导数修正（特别是三次Lovelock项）是否改变近极值黑洞软模计数，进而影响熵的log T修正项系数，这是理解量子引力对黑洞微观统计和热力学普适性的关键问题。

Method: 在七维三阶Lovelock引力框架下，分析近极值、带电、慢转动黑洞解；通过冻结Maxwell模、处理S^5上15个转动模作为量子转子，结合热力学与量子转动谱分析log T系数的温度依赖行为；并引入探针带电标量场，解析推导近视界Schwinger产生阈值。

Result: 1) 软模多重性不变，但有效自由度受温度调控：Maxwell模冻结，S^5转动模在K_J^{-1}以上才激发；2) log T系数从低温3/2增至高温9，跳过高斯值19/2；3) Lovelock耦合仅移动交叉温度K_J^{-1}，不改变模数；4) 大AdS黑洞存在宽温区使转动贡献与经典熵竞争；5) 得到三次耦合依赖的Schwinger阈值，并给出其大AdS极限下与BF界无违反共存的条件。

Conclusion: 高阶导数引力不改变软模数量，但显著修改其热激发行为与熵的量子修正结构；log T系数具有非平凡温度依赖性和模型依赖性，挑战了基于高斯近似的普适性预期；结果强调了背景几何尺度（如AdS半径）与高阶耦合协同决定低能有效自由度的重要性。

Abstract: Near-extremal black holes are controlled by a small set of soft modes, and counting these modes fixes the coefficient of $\log T$ in the entropy. We ask whether genuine six-derivative interactions change that count, working in seven-dimensional third-order Lovelock gravity, the simplest setting where the cubic Lovelock term is dynamical. The multiplicities stay the same, but the naive count fails. At fixed charge the Maxwell mode is frozen out, and the fifteen rotational modes of the $S^5$ act as a quantum rotor that only switches on above the scale $K_J^{-1}$. As a result, the coefficient of $\log T$ runs from $3/2$ at the lowest temperatures to $9$ above that scale, and never reaches the naive Gaussian value $19/2$. We compute the moment of inertia $K_J$ from the charged slow-rotation solution and find that the Lovelock couplings move the crossover without changing the multiplicities. Only large AdS black holes have a wide window in which the full rotational contribution competes with the classical entropy. Adding a probe charged scalar, we derive an analytic near-horizon Schwinger threshold in the cubic coupling. Its large-AdS limit tells us when the broad rotational crossover window can coexist with the absence of a charged-scalar Breitenlohner--Freedman (BF) bound violation.

</details>


### [118] [Interpolating between 4d $\mathcal{N}= 1$ SCFT supercurrent multiplet anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04967)
*Pantelis Panopoulos,Ioannis Papadimitriou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维N=1超共形场论中两种不同背景超引力（共形超引力与Ferrara-Zumino超引力）所定义的电流多重态之间的关系，通过构造一个显式的局域配分函数反项，建立了二者之间的联系，并指出该反项包含超出已有超空间形式的新分量项，对物理观测量的比较至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同超引力背景下定义的超共形流多重态（如共形与Ferrara-Zumino多重态）之间的关系，及其对应的不同超共形反常结构。

Method: 将共形超引力配分函数通过Wess-Zumino作用提升为旧极小超引力背景场的泛函，并推导出连接两种方案的显式局域反项（以分量形式给出）。

Result: 得到了一个完整的局域反项，它不仅包含依赖于旧极小辅助标量的已知超空间反项部分，还包含新的、不依赖该辅助标量的额外项；这些新项对两种多重态下物理观测量的比较起关键作用。

Conclusion: 共形与Ferrara-Zumino多重态可通过一个含新分量结构的局域反项相互关联，揭示了超共形反常在不同超引力实现间的非平凡对应关系。

Abstract: A renormalized SCFT can be coupled to a number of background off-shell supergravity multiplets, defining different current multiplets and superconformal anomalies. We focus on the relation between the conformal and Ferrara-Zumino multiplets of generic $\mathcal{N}= 1$ SCFTs in four dimensions, defined respectively by $\mathcal{N}= 1$ conformal and old-minimal supergravity. We show that the conformal super-gravity partition function, when lifted to a functional of the old-minimal background fields through a suitable Wess-Zumino action, is related to the old-minimal partition function by a local counterterm that we determine explicitly in components. This counterterm interpolates between two different old-minimal supergravity schemes with different superconformal anomalies. Part of the counterterm depends on the old-minimal auxiliary scalar and provides the explicit component form of the super-space counterterm constructed in [1]. However, the full counterterm that interpolates between the two multiplets contains additional terms, independent of the old-minimal auxiliary scalar, which are not present in this superspace counterterm. These additional terms play a key role in the comparison of physical observables between the two multiplets.

</details>


### [119] [Weyl Rescalings: A First Order Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04978)
*L. Andrianopoli,J. Zanelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在极高能量下Weyl变换（局域尺度变换）作为自然对称性的角色，及其在低能下的自发破缺；通过考虑挠率的作用，解决了Cartan第一类引力形式（使用vielbein和独立Lorentz联络）与第二类度规形式在Weyl对称性表现上的张力，并阐明了物质场与引力的共形耦合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weyl变换在高能下应是基本对称性，但在两种引力形式（Cartan第一类与度规第二类）中其表现不一致，尤其在Ricci张量和联络的Weyl行为上产生张力，需澄清其等价性及挠率的作用。

Method: 基于Cartan第一阶形式，分析vielbein、Lorentz联络与挠率在Weyl变换下的行为，比较其与度规形式的差异，重点考察挠率非零时对联络动力学和曲率变换的影响。

Result: 证明挠率的存在可协调两类形式的Weyl行为：当挠率参与动力学时，Lorentz联络可获得Weyl协变性，从而消解张力；该机制自然导出物质场与引力的共形耦合形式。

Conclusion: 挠率是调和Cartan形式与度规形式Weyl对称性表述的关键；Weyl不变性要求在第一阶形式中必须恰当地纳入挠率，这对理解共形引力与标准模型耦合具有基础意义。

Abstract: Weyl transformations, --local rescalings in which the metric transforms as $g_{μν}(x)\longrightarrow Ω^2(x)\, g_{μν}(x)$--, are expected to be a symmetry of nature at extremely high energy, that should be (spontaneously) broken at low energy. In Cartan's first order formulation of gravity this translates into the condition that the local frames (vielbein) scale as $e^a(x) \longrightarrow Ω(x) e^a(x)$. In this construction, the Lorentz connection one-form fields $ω^a{}_b$ are independent from $e^a$, and since Lorentz transformations and dilatations commute, $ω$ and $R(ω)$ must be Weyl invariant, while in the second order metric formalism the Ricci tensor undergoes a non-homogeneous transformation. This generates a tension between the two formulations, which should be equivalent in the vanishing torsion limit. Here we show how the puzzle can be resolved taking into account the role of torsion. This also sheds some light on the conformal coupling of matter fields to gravity.

</details>


### [120] [Hecke Relations from Self-Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05002)
*Justin Kaidi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过不对称轨道（asymmetric orbifolds）的自对偶性，为Hecke算子在不同中心荷的有理共形场论（RCFT）字符之间的联系提供物理解释，并推广了经典的Harvey-Wu构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hecke算子能关联不同中心荷的有理共形场论字符，但其物理意义长期不明。

Method: 利用不对称轨道的自对偶性解释Hecke算子的物理含义，并构造更广义的字符恒等式。

Result: 给出了Hecke算子关系的清晰物理解释，并展示了超越Harvey-Wu构造的新恒等式示例。

Conclusion: Hecke算子关系本质上源于自对偶不对称轨道，这为RCFT字符结构提供了新的几何与对称性视角。

Abstract: Hecke operators can be used to relate characters of rational conformal field theories with different central charges, but it has remained a mystery how to interpret these relations physically. We offer a simple explanation in terms of self-duality under asymmetric orbifolds. Self-dual orbifolds also give more general identities beyond the familiar Harvey-Wu construction, which we illustrate through examples.

</details>


### [121] [Horizon exchange and the universal all-spin response of Kerr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05054)
*Pierre Vanhove*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi（MST）近区响应之间精确的单值性关系，该关系源于径向Teukolsky方程在内外视界交换下的不变性，并由此得到引力扰动下四极吸收中视界交换奇部的贡献，同时将交换偶部动力学响应组织为digamma函数形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 径向Teukolsky方程具有内外视界交换对称性，但物理边界条件不具此对称性，因此需探究该对称性如何体现在散射/吸收响应中。

Method: 利用径向Teukolsky方程的视界交换不变性，推导出MST近区解之间的精确单值性关系，并在低频极限及任意自旋下展开分析。

Result: 获得各多极矩下主导阶的视界交换奇响应；确定引力四极吸收中交换奇部贡献；将交换偶部响应统一表达为digamma函数，其展开自然重现Kerr-Compton计算中的奇偶zeta结构。

Conclusion: 精确单值性关系本身是核心成果，优于以往仅适用于小自旋的系数展开结果。

Abstract: The radial Teukolsky equation is invariant under exchange of the outer and inner horizons, although the physical boundary condition is not. We derive an exact monodromy relation between the corresponding Mano--Suzuki--Takasugi (MST) near-zone responses. At leading order in frequency and to all orders in spin, this relation yields, for every multipole, an expression for the leading horizon-exchange-odd response. For gravitational perturbations, it determines the exchange-odd contribution to quadrupolar absorption and organizes the exchange-even dynamical response into a digamma function whose expansion accounts for the odd- and even-zeta structures found in Kerr-Compton calculations. The exact relation, rather than the individual previously known small-spin coefficients, is the central result.

</details>


### [122] [A Holographic Weyl Semimetal in an Effective Gauss--Bonnet Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05182)
*F. Mousavi,K. Bitaghsir Fadafan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯-博内变形对Weyl半金属与绝缘相竞争的影响，发现正耦合扩大导电相区域但未必增强电响应，而零温相变点不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有效高斯-博内变形如何影响探针D3/D7模型中Weyl半金属导电相与绝缘味相之间的热力学竞争。

Method: 基于horizon-reaching与smoothly capped D7膜嵌入分别表征导电与绝缘相，通过自由能分析一阶相变，并结合on-shell几何分析分解有限温度效应。

Result: 正高斯-博内耦合使共存线移向更大味质量、扩展Weyl半金属热力学优势区域；负耦合作用相反；零温归一化相变点不变；导电性响应（如纵向与反常霍尔电导跃变）未必增强，甚至减弱或符号反转。

Conclusion: 几何变形不仅改变哪一相在热力学上更受青睐，还显著改变Weyl半金属相的输运性质，表明相稳定性与电输运响应可解耦。

Abstract: We study how an effective Gauss--Bonnet deformation of the thermal geometry changes the competition between the conducting Weyl-semimetal and insulating flavour phases of the probe D3/D7 model. The phases are represented by horizon-reaching and smoothly capped D7 embeddings, respectively, whose free energies exhibit a first-order transition. At the temperatures examined, positive Gauss--Bonnet coupling shifts coexistence toward larger flavour mass and expands the thermodynamically preferred Weyl-semimetal region, while negative coupling shifts the boundary in the opposite direction. The normalized zero-temperature transition remains unchanged. An on-shell analysis shows that the leading finite-temperature shift is largely explained by an effective rescaling of the thermal scale, opposed by an intrinsic fixed-horizon geometric contribution. The enlargement of the conducting phase does not imply stronger electrical response: the longitudinal and anomalous Hall conductivity jumps at coexistence can decrease, and the coupling response of the longitudinal jump reverses with temperature. The mass-dependent maximum of the longitudinal DC conductivity can also cease to occur on the thermodynamically preferred branch. Thus the geometric deformation changes both which phase is favored and how the Weyl-semimetal phase conducts.

</details>


### [123] [From Z to all: cyclic sum rules for thermal CFT partition functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05281)
*Arnav Advant,Harsh Anand,Nathan Benjamin,Abhijit Gadde,Vipul Kumar,Shiraz Minwalla,Jyotirmoy Mukherjee,Sridip Pal,Asikur Rahaman,Pabitra Ray*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导出偶维共形场论（CFT_d, d=2κ）在扭曲S^{d-1}×S^1几何上高温展开的普适约束，并将其统一为一个关于Z_{κ+1}大共形微分同胚对称性的单一方程，该方程指出ln Z/β的Z_{κ+1}-不变部分为由a-反常决定的常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示偶维共形场论在扭曲球面×圆柱几何下高温展开所满足的深层普适结构和对称性约束。

Method: 基于嵌入空间的代数形式主义，结合CP不变性和热有效作用量的局域性假设，推导高温展开的无穷塔普适约束，并将其整合为Z_{κ+1}对称性下的单一恒等式。

Our equation asserts that the $\mathbb{Z}_{κ+1}$-invariant part of $\ln Z/β$ is a constant fixed by the a-anomaly. Abstract: We construct boundary Liouville conformal field theory at central charge $c\leq1$ with FZZT-type boundary conditions and show that it satisfies the Cardy--Lewellen sewing constraints on the disk and the annulus. Using the analytic bootstrap we derive the so far missing boundary three-point and bulk-to-boundary structure constants and a consistent disk one-point function as explicit meromorphic functions of the Liouville momenta. In sharp contrast to $c\geq25$, the open-string spectrum between two ``timelike'' FZZT boundaries is \emph{discrete}, yet non-trivially consistent through open-closed duality with the \emph{continuous} closed-string spectrum of Ribault and Santachiara.

Method: 采用适配渐近零边界的‘前瞻性’双零规范，结合自举论证，建立加权能量估计，融合局域积分能量衰减、事件视界附近的红移估计及朝向零无穷远的rp估计；关键创新在于对旋量分量进行适当重加权以闭合估计。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the nonlinear stability of the Schwarzschild exterior for the spherically symmetric Einstein-massless Dirac system. More precisely, we consider sufficiently small perturbations of Schwarzschild characteristic initial data and prove that the corresponding maximal development remains globally controlled in the exterior region (orbital stability), and converges, in a suitable teleological double-null gauge, to a Schwarzschild spacetime with a final mass (asymptotic stability). In particular, the maximal future development contains a complete future null infinity and a regular event horizon, with a nonempty black hole region in its interior. The main difficulties in the proof arise from the delicate coupling between the Dirac operator and the geometry, compared with the scalar wave counterpart, as well as from the failure of the Dirac stress-energy tensor to satisfy a positive energy condition. To overcome these difficulties, we rely on a bootstrap argument coupling the geometry and the Dirac field. Working in suitable teleological double-null gauges adapted to the asymptotic null boundaries, we establish weighted energy estimates combining integrated local energy decay, red-shift estimates near the event horizon, and an rp-estimates towards null infinity. A core ingredient is the use of suitable reweightings of the spinor components, which allow us to close these estimates despite the nonlinear coupling to the geometry.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a gauge-invariant teleparallel framework in which the universality of the gravitational coupling is relaxed for a scalar field, while the gravitational sector retains the form of the teleparallel equivalent of General Relativity. The non-universal interaction is encoded in a scalar-sector tetrad whose coupling to the universal translational gauge potential is controlled by a field-dependent gravitational charge. A Stückelberg completion preserves local translational gauge invariance, and we derive the gravitational, scalar-field, and Stückelberg equations, together with the antisymmetric constraint and their consistency relations. As a first application, we investigate homogeneous and isotropic cosmology, where the Stückelberg constraint provides a first integral governing the evolution of the non-universal coupling. In the kination-dominated regime, the standard stiff-fluid behavior is recovered asymptotically, whereas in the potential-dominated regime, accelerated expansion requires additional conditions beyond potential domination. We identify compatible expanding solutions in which departures from universality are dynamically suppressed, including examples approaching power-law inflation or asymptotically de Sitter expansion. Including a universally coupled perfect fluid and a constant scalar potential, we obtain a late-time solution exhibiting a subdominant matter-like contribution to the background Friedmann equation and an asymptotic de Sitter limit. These results provide a gauge-theoretic realization of non-universal scalar couplings and highlight their potential implications for early- and late-time cosmology.

</details>


### [127] [Reference-renormalized Jacobi primitives for finite-distance massive-particle deflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03746)
*Reggie C. Pantig*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参考归一化的Jacobi-本原高斯-博内形式主义，用于静态球对称时空中有限距离弱偏折问题，避免了对类时圆轨道的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖于类时圆轨道来固定曲率本原的加性常数，但在某些时空（如无稳定圆轨道、Jacobi度规非黎曼或参考几何非欧几何时）该方法失效，因此需要一种不依赖圆轨道的更普适方法。

Method: 采用固定能量的Jacobi度规描述类时测地线，将径向曲率本原定义为仅差一加性常数的量；不以圆轨道为基准归零，而是减去同能量下的参考Jacobi几何，并对端点进行归一化处理，从而得到不含圆轨道假设的曲率贡献表达式。

Result: 证明了新方法与传统圆轨道归一化方法在两者均适用时结果一致；且新方法可推广至无圆轨道、Jacobi域外或非欧参考几何等情形；并在bumblebee、Kottler和JNW三种典型时空上验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 圆轨道锚定只是曲率本原的一种辅助规范选择，而非有限距离Jacobi高斯-博内偏折分析的先决条件；该形式主义提升了理论的普适性与物理清晰性。

Abstract: We develop a reference-renormalized Jacobi-primitive Gauss-Bonnet formalism for finite-distance weak deflection of massive particles in static, spherically symmetric spacetimes. The construction uses the fixed-energy Jacobi metric associated with timelike geodesics and treats the radial curvature primitive as a quantity defined only up to an additive constant. Instead of fixing this constant by requiring the primitive to vanish at a circular timelike orbit, we subtract a reference Jacobi geometry at the same conserved particle energy and impose endpoint normalization on the curvature-density discrepancy. This gives a circular-orbit-free primitive representation of the Gauss-Bonnet curvature contribution while preserving the operational finite-distance definition of the bending angle through local source and receiver directions. We prove that the reference-normalized and circular-orbit-normalized prescriptions agree whenever both are admissible, since their primitives differ only by a constant that cancels from the exact curvature strip integral. The method remains applicable when no admissible circular timelike orbit exists, when the orbit lies outside the Riemannian Jacobi domain, or when the natural no-lens comparison geometry is non-Euclidean. We demonstrate the formalism in three representative settings: Schwarzschild-like bumblebee, Kottler, and Janis-Newman-Winicour (JNW) spacetimes. The formalism therefore identifies circular-orbit anchoring as an auxiliary primitive gauge choice rather than a prerequisite for finite-distance Jacobi Gauss-Bonnet deflection.

</details>

Abstract: We study circular null orbits, thermodynamic topology, and the holographic thermodynamic description of a four-dimensional magnetically charged AdS black hole in nonlinear electrodynamics. The background orbit structure is classified using Duan s phi-mapping construction and compared with the extraordinary electromagnetic characteristics. The latter defines an effective optical metric with an additional radial factor that modifies the orbit condition and introduces explicit dependence on the AdS radius. For four representative configurations, the local indices of the five isolated orbits are unchanged while their radii shift by up to 5.187 %. The background mass thresholds vary smoothly across sigma = 1/4 when the total mass is used, whereas the fixed-m0 parametrization is singular there. Regularity at the center requires sigma > 1 and m0 = 0. The thermodynamic analysis separates local stability indices from equilibrium coexistence: stable Gibbs-free-energy branches cross only in model-dependent parameter intervals. The boundary canonical ensemble exhibits critical behavior consistent with mean-field exponents, and the examined grand-canonical ensemble has a Hawking-Page crossing. In the mixed ensemble, the free-energy branches have a finite endpoint discontinuity, but the sampled competing states do not satisfy the full local-stability criterion

</details>

Consequently, the stationary, asymptotically flat, $I^+$-regular, analytic vacuum black hole uniqueness conjecture is settled affirmatively in full.

Conclusion: QNM表示的收敛性由复时间反弹奇点控制，Kerr时空因自转导致两类QNM及其不同收敛行为，该结论可推广至带电黑洞时空。

Abstract: We show that the convergence region of the QNM representation of the retarded Green's function of the Kerr spacetime is set by complex-time bouncing singularities, as in our previous work for the Schwarzschild case. The main difference between Kerr and Schwarzschild is that there are two distinct QNM families, prolate and oblate, which have different convergence regions. We provide explicit analytical formulae for the bounce radii and corresponding convergence regions in Boyer-Lindquist coordinates. We also provide an analysis of convergence in the Reissner-Nordström spacetime.

</details>

A perturbative analysis further shows that nonextremal Reissner-Nordström black holes do not support regular, exponentially localized, spherically symmetric electroweak clouds satisfying such condition. Thus, the hairy solutions cannot emerge via a bifurcation from this electrovacuum family. Moreover, our numerical investigation reveals two apparently disconnected families of bosonic stars. Within the explored parameter space, the hairy black holes are connected to the strongly gravitating one.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需额外施加辐射条件来选择物理解，且处理静态 m=0 模和强场高自旋情形存在困难；本文旨在通过超曲面切片与正则性准则自然选取推迟解，并提升数值稳定性与精度。

Method: 采用抵达事件视界与未来零无穷远的超曲面切片求解频域 m 模 Teukolsky 方程；对点粒子源使用 m 模去奇/有效源方案；将二维椭圆问题用四区域伪谱法求解，并通过对称性破缺与偶/奇分解处理赤道对称性。
