Abstract: We solve the frequency-domain, $m$-mode Teukolsky equation for the spin-weight $s=-2$ Weyl scalar $ψ_4$ sourced by a point mass on a circular, equatorial orbit around a Kerr black hole. The equation is posed on hyperboloidal slices that reach the event horizon and future null infinity, so that the physical (retarded) solution is selected by regularity alone, without imposing radiation conditions. The particle is treated with the $m$-mode puncture/effective-source scheme, and the resulting two-dimensional elliptic problem is solved with a four-domain pseudo-spectral method in which the equatorial symmetry is broken and handled by an even/odd decomposition of the field. We test the method for orbital radius $r_0=10M$ and spins up to $|a|/M=0.9$, covering prograde and retrograde orbits. The energy fluxes to infinity agree with independent Black Hole Perturbation Toolkit results to $\sim10^{-7}$ for the dominant modes and to better than $2\times10^{-5}$ for all modes up to $m=20$ at moderate resolutions. The static $m=0$ mode requires no special treatment.

</details>


### [133] [Kerr metric perturbations for second-order self-force theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03888)
*Benjamin Leather,Samuel D. Upton,Patrick Bourg,Kevin O. Cunningham,Sam R. Dolan,Conor Dyson,Zachary Nasipak,Nami Nishimura,Andrew Spiers,Maarten van de Meent,Barry Wardell*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了在Kerr时空内沿赤道圆轨道运动的点质量所引起的首阶度规扰动，满足二阶自作用理论所需的高精度、全贡献、规范兼容性及高效计算等要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足二阶自作用理论对首阶度规扰动的严格要求（如高精度、包含黑洞质量与角动量扰动、Lorenz规范近粒子处、Bondi-Sachs规范远场、渐近平直等），发展一套可靠且普适的首阶扰动计算框架。

Method: 基于可分离的Teukolsky型方程，将扰动按自旋加权类球谐模式（ℓ,m）分解，在双曲面切片上谱方法求解；引入新型穿刺函数处理近粒子处缓慢收敛问题，实现m模高精度（~10⁻¹⁰）提取。

Result: 获得覆盖Kerr自旋-0.9 ≤ a/M ≤ 0.9、轨道半径至50M的高精度首阶度规扰动，满足Lorenz规范（近粒子）与近Bondi-Sachs规范（远场），各m模误差约10⁻¹⁰，适用于二阶自作用及其他Kerr扰动相关研究。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了首个兼具高精度、规范一致性、全域高效性与物理完备性的Kerr时空首阶度规扰动数值方案，为二阶自作用计算奠定关键基础，并拓展至环境效应与准正规模等研究。

Abstract: We present a calculation of the first-order metric perturbation arising from a point mass moving on a circular, equatorial orbit in Kerr spacetime. The metric perturbation is specifically aimed at meeting the requirements of second-order self-force theory: it is highly accurate; incorporates all contributions, including those associated with perturbations to the black hole's mass and angular momentum; is in Lorenz gauge near the worldline and in (near) Bondi--Sachs gauge at large radius; and can be efficiently evaluated across the entire exterior spacetime. The Lorenz gauge gives a well-understood singular structure at the particle, while the (near) Bondi--Sachs gauge ensures the asymptotic fall-off needed for an asymptotically flat second-order source. Our formalism reconstructs the metric from separable Teukolsky-type equations, decomposed into spin-weighted spheroidal harmonic modes and solved spectrally on hyperboloidal slices. The resulting $(\ell,m)$-mode sums converge rapidly across most of the domain, but slowly near the point mass; we overcome this with a novel use of a puncture to extract accurate $m$-mode data, yielding output accurate to $\sim 10^{-10}$ per $m$-mode and suitable for use at second order. Our metric-perturbation results cover a range of Kerr spins $-0.9 \le a/M \le 0.9$ and orbital radii out to $50M$. In addition to its immediate application in second-order self-force calculations, our results can also be used as input to other calculations involving Kerr metric perturbations, including studies of environmental effects and quasinormal modes.

</details>


### [134] [Quasi-Normal Modes of Covariant Loop Quantum Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03902)
*Chao Zhang,Sushant G. Ghosh,Anzhong Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在协变圈量子黑洞背景下的无质量标量场的准正规模（QNMs）和时域响应，通过引入高斯局域化全纯轮廓来实现势能的局部变形，并分析了这种变形对频谱和波形演化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索协变圈量子黑洞背景下标量场的准正规模和时域响应，以理解局域全纯修正如何影响引力波信号的可探测性。

Method: 采用频率域射击法、时域演化、矩阵 pencil 模式提取和波形重构等方法，结合高斯局域化全纯轮廓建模势能变形。

Result: 发现准正规模随势垒位置变化呈螺旋轨迹，高阶模更敏感，并出现模式超越现象；早期响应接近施瓦西情形，后期差异显著；定义了局域波形偏差时间和相对振幅微弱时间以界定可观测窗口。

Conclusion: 局域全纯修正显著影响频谱结构与波形演化，为未来引力扰动研究及引力波可探测性评估提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) and time-domain response of a massless, minimally coupled test scalar field on static, spherically symmetric covariant loop quantum black hole (cLQBH) backgrounds. Focusing on a phenomenological, Gaussian-localized holonomy profile, we obtain a localized deformation of the scalar effective potential containing both positive and negative parts. This provides a geometric realization of the bump effect, with the potential deformation and tortoise-coordinate mapping determined consistently by the same background. We characterize the deformation by its location, amplitude, and width, and study its effects using frequency-domain shooting and time-domain evolution, supplemented by matrix-pencil mode extraction and waveform reconstruction. The QNM spectrum exhibits spiral trajectories as the bump location varies, enhanced sensitivity of the overtones, and mode overtaking, whereby the branch continued from the first Schwarzschild overtone becomes longer-lived than the continued fundamental branch. Despite substantial spectral changes, the early response can remain close to its Schwarzschild counterpart, with more pronounced differences emerging later. To quantify this behavior, we introduce a local waveform-deviation time and compare it with a relative-amplitude faintness time, thereby defining an operational observation window. The deviation time depends nonmonotonically on the bump location, and suitable parameter choices allow appreciable departures to emerge before the adopted faintness threshold is reached. These results connect localized holonomy corrections with spectral sensitivity and waveform evolution, providing a basis for future gravitational-perturbation studies and assessments of gravitational-wave detectability.

</details>


### [135] [Noisy pipelines II: Non-parametric Noise Inference for LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03911)
*Charlie Hoy,Joseph Bayley,Ollie Burke,Laura K. Nuttall*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需先验知识的非参数方法，用于建模LISA空间引力波探测器的仪器噪声协方差矩阵，通过样条与逆洛伦兹函数组合拟合功率谱与互谱密度，在7天模拟纯噪声数据上验证了其白化效果和适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA的仪器噪声高度相关，传统对角化频域噪声建模失效；准确建模噪声对信号提取、参数估计和天体物理推断至关重要。

Method: 采用样条函数与逆洛伦兹函数组合，对LISA全噪声协方差矩阵进行非参数拟合，直接从模拟噪声数据中估计功率谱密度（PSD）和互谱密度（CSD）。

Result: 在7天模拟噪声数据上成功估计PSD和CSD；中位数估计结果能将数据白化至接近单位正态分布；模型可融入全局拟合流程，并辅助识别未知噪声成分的频率依赖特性。

Conclusion: 该非参数噪声建模方法不依赖LISA先验仪器知识，具备实际应用潜力，可作为现有参数化建模的补充，提升LISA数据中弱引力波信号的检测与参数估计精度。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is expected to observe gravitational-wave signals from massive black hole binaries, extreme mass ratio inspirals, and more. Despite the significant scientific potential, accurately extracting these signals from the data will only be possible if LISA's instrumental noise is understood: poor noise modelling will result in lost signals, biased parameter estimates, and incorrect astrophysical insights. Modelling LISA's instrumental noise is more challenging than its ground-based counterpart since the data is highly correlated, meaning that the noise covariance matrix can no longer be assumed diagonal in frequency and in the different data streams. In this work, we present an initial approach to perform non-parametric noise inference for LISA that requires no prior knowledge of the LISA observatory. By fitting the full noise covariance matrix with a combination of splines and inverse Lorentzians, we show that our model can estimate the power and cross spectral densities for LISA from 7 days of simulated noise-only data. We find that the median estimate from our non-parametric model whitens the data according to the expected unit normal distribution. We envision that our non-parametric model can be used as part of the global fit, as well as in combination with modelled approaches to identify, quantify, and constrain the frequency dependence of unknown noise artifacts.

</details>


### [136] [Multimessenger astrophysics drives the case for an Australian Gravitational-Wave observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04150)
*Fiona H. Panther,Paul D. Lasky,Nikhil Sarin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在澳大利亚建设下一代引力波观测站的科学价值，重点分析其在多信使天文学中的作用，表明其每年可支持探测多达8个引力波-千新星联合事件，并显著提升定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澳大利亚具备历史贡献、大型科学项目经验及独特地理优势，有望成为全球引力波观测网络的关键节点；本文旨在量化其在多信使天文学中的潜在科学贡献。

Method: 通过模拟分析澳大利亚引力波探测器在第二代（2G）和第三代（3G）全球观测网络中的性能，评估其对引力波-千新星多信使事件的探测率与天空定位精度（1–10 deg²）的影响。

Result: 澳大利亚观测站可在2G和3G网络中每年支持探测约8个引力波+千新星事件，定位精度达1–10 deg²；即使仅具2G灵敏度，在XG网络中仍能显著推动多信使科学研究。

Conclusion: 澳大利亚建设引力波观测站具有明确且重要的科学价值，尤其在提升多信使天文学能力方面贡献突出，应纳入更广泛的国家战略考量。

Abstract: The next generation of an international network of gravitational-wave observatories is now in the advanced planning stages. With Australia's substantial history of contributions in this field, its history of scientific mega-projects like the Square Kilometer Array, and its unique geographic location and conditions, it is ideally placed to host an observatory in the global network. It has already been demonstrated that a gravitational wave detector in Australia plays an important role in minimizing the sky localization uncertainty of gravitational wave events. We study a particular science case for an Australian observatory, focusing on its potential contributions to multimessenger astronomy. We demonstrate that an observatory in Australia can enable the detection of up to eight multimessenger gravitational-wave plus kilonova events per year, with localisations better than $1-10°^2$ in both second- and third-generation networks. We show that, in a global network of XG observatories, an Australian observatory with 2G sensitivity would still enable significant advances in multimessenger science. The full case for an Australian observatory must be considered in a much broader context; however, this work indicates it could make substantial contributions to multimessenger astrophysics.

</details>


### [137] [Binary black hole mergers in massive Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03913)
*Harry L. H. Shum,Llibert Aresté Saló,Miguel Bezares,Thomas P. Sotiriou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有线性高斯-博内耦合函数的爱因斯坦-标量-高斯-博内（EsGB）引力理论中，完全非线性的双黑洞并合过程；针对该理论缺乏成熟初始数据求解器的问题，作者发现传统用于无质量标量场的方法会导致‘垃圾辐射’污染晚期动力学，并提出通过早期修改运动方程来抑制该问题，从而成功模拟并合全过程中的标量场演化；初步分析显示，标量四极模的ringdown结构丰富，早期受自旋-2污染频率主导，晚期则由标量质量决定的频率主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EsGB引力中缺乏适配的初始数据求解器，而沿用无质量标量场的常规方法会引入严重污染晚期动力学的‘垃圾辐射’，阻碍对真实标量场并合动力学及ringdown特征的研究。

Method: 提出一种在数值模拟早期阶段有针对性地修改运动方程的方法，以压制由不恰当初始数据引发的 junk radiation；在此基础上开展全非线性双黑洞并合的数值相对论模拟，并分析标量与张量模式ringdown的频谱特征。

Result: 成功实现了EsGB理论中带质量标量场的双黑洞并合数值模拟；发现标量四极模ringdown呈现两阶段频谱演化：早期以自旋-2污染频率为主，晚期转为由标量质量决定的本征频率主导。

Conclusion: 该工作为在强场、非线性区域研究EsGB等扩展引力理论提供了可行的数值策略；揭示了标量场ringdown对理论参数（如标量质量）的高度敏感性，为未来引力波观测甄别新物理提供了关键理论依据。

Abstract: We study fully non-linear binary black hole mergers in massive Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet (EsGB) gravity with a linear Gauss-Bonnet coupling function. In the absence of a well-developed initial data solver for EsGB gravity, we demonstrate that the conventional workaround solution for massless scalar fields results in confined junk radiation that contaminates late-time dynamics in the massive theory. We propose a solution to this problem by suitably modifying the equations of motion at the early stages of the simulation to tame such junk radiation, thereby allowing us to study the dynamics of the scalar field during the process of a black hole merger. This is followed by a preliminary analysis of the frequency content in the ringdown of the scalar and tensor modes. In particular, the quadrupole scalar mode exhibits a rich ringdown structure: while it is dominated by the spin-2 ``contamination'' frequency in the early stages, its late time morphology becomes primarily determined by the scalar mass frequency.

</details>


### [138] [A Unified AI Framework for Rapid and Accurate Waveform Generation of Compact Binary Coalescences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04713)
*Mengfei Sun,Jin Li,Yuanhong Zhong,Jie Wu,Borui Wang,Xianghe Ma,Minghui Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合物理建模与深度学习的统一框架，用于快速、高精度生成各类致密双星并合（BBH、BNS、NSBH）的引力波波形，覆盖长周期、多物理效应，且比传统方法快约1800倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三代引力波探测器将观测到大量长时程致密双星并合信号，亟需快速、准确的波形生成方法以支撑天体物理研究。

Method: 构建融合解析残差谱表示、共享学习和按双星类型解码的统一深度学习框架，支持自旋进动、高阶模式、物质效应及中子星并合后辐射等物理效应。

Result: 生成波形覆盖数百至数千周期，与参考波形平均失配率介于5.89×10⁻⁴至5.34×10⁻²；批处理速度最大提升约1800倍。

Conclusion: 该框架为所有致密双星并合提供高效、高保真、统一的波形生成工具，显著提升未来引力波数据分析能力。

Abstract: The astrophysical discovery potential of future gravitational wave detectors will hinge on accurate and rapid waveform generation. Third generation detectors are expected to observe many long duration compact binary coalescence signals that can probe binary formation and evolution as well as the nature of matter under extreme conditions. We develop a unified framework that combines physical modeling with deep learning. The framework uses an analytic residual spectral representation, shared learning, and decoders for each binary type. It generates waveforms for binary black holes, binary neutron stars, and neutron star black hole binaries, providing a single unified generator for all compact binary coalescences. The generated waveforms include the physical effects described by each reference model, such as spin precession, higher order modes, matter effects, and postmerger radiation for binary neutron stars. The waveforms span hundreds to thousands of cycles, with mean mismatches ranging from $5.89\times10^{-4}$ to $5.34\times10^{-2}$ relative to the reference waveforms. Batch generation achieves a maximum speedup of about 1800 over reference waveform generation.

</details>


### [139] [Imaginary Time as a Complex Representation of Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04372)
*William McConville*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于虚时间坐标 $X^0 = ict$ 的洛伦兹相对论的系统性复表示，将其视为可逆的表示变换而非威克转动；该框架在复向量空间中嵌入实物理时空，引入复双线性度规与厄米度规，并统一处理相对论动力学、广义相对论、波方程及非相对论极限，揭示了虚时间的几何本质与多领域间的结构性联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清虚时间坐标 $ict$ 的几何意义，避免将其简单视为威克转动（通往欧氏时空的技巧），而是在保持物理内容不变前提下，建立一个内在一致、可逆的复表示框架，以揭示相对论几何、作用量原理、波动传播与解析延拓之间的深层联系。

Method: 将时间坐标设为纯虚数 $X^0 = ict$，把物理时空视为某个复向量空间的实子空间；在此基础上定义复双线性度规和厄米度规，二者在物理子空间上均退化为原洛伦兹度规；将四维动力学量（如固有时、四动量、哈密顿-雅可比方程）、广义相对论核心结构（度规、联络、曲率、爱因斯坦方程、守恒律）以及相对论性波动方程（含非相对论极限）全部系统地推广至该复表示；通过Kerr与FLRW时空的具体计算验证其自洽性。

Result: 成功构建了一个等价于标准洛伦兹相对论的复表示体系：所有物理可观测量和方程（包括弯曲时空中的爱因斯坦方程与守恒律）保持原有形式与内容；Kerr与FLRW解被自然容纳；薛定谔方程及其与欧氏时间演化的联系在该框架下自然浮现；未引入新物理预言，但明确了虚时间的几何角色并揭示了多个理论模块间的统一结构。

Conclusion: 虚时间坐标 $ict$ 可被严格理解为一种可逆的复表示选择，而非仅用于路径积分的临时技巧；该复表示不仅完全等价于标准相对论，而且提供了更统一的几何与分析视角，架起了相对论、经典作用量、量子波动与解析延拓之间的概念桥梁。

Abstract: We develop a systematic complex representation of Lorentzian relativity based on the imaginary temporal coordinate $X^0=ict$. Rather than treating this substitution as a Wick rotation to Euclidean spacetime, we formulate it as an invertible change of representation in which physical spacetime is identified with a real subspace of a complex vector space. This construction introduces two related metric structures: a complex-bilinear form and a Hermitian form, both of which reproduce the original Lorentzian interval when restricted to physical spacetime. We extend the representation to relativistic dynamics, including proper time, four-momentum, and the Hamilton-Jacobi formulation, and show that the metric, connection, curvature tensors, Einstein equations, and conservation laws retain their conventional content in curved spacetime. Explicit applications to the Kerr and Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker geometries demonstrate consistency for rotating and time-dependent spacetimes. We further examine relativistic wave equations and their nonrelativistic limit, showing how the Schrödinger equation and its relation to Euclidean-time evolution emerge within the same complex temporal framework. The resulting formulation introduces no new physical predictions, but clarifies the geometric role of imaginary temporal coordinates and reveals structural connections among relativistic geometry, action, wave propagation, and analytic continuation.

</details>


### [140] [Single-horizon regular black holes and the fate of the inner-horizon instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04385)
*Chen Lan,Hao Yang,Maxim Khlopov,Maxim Krasnov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型单视界正则黑洞（RBH），证明了正则性并不要求双视界结构，并建立了视界奇偶性定理；构造了两类具有有限面积核心的单视界RBH，分析其物质内容、能量条件与热力学性质，并验证其对标量扰动的模式稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统正则黑洞通常具有双视界结构，其中内柯西视界易受质量膨胀不稳定性影响；本文旨在探索是否存在满足正则性但无内视界的黑洞构型，以规避该经典不稳定性。

Method: 基于静态球对称时空和渐近静态外区假设，推导并证明‘视界奇偶性定理’；构造两类具有有限面积核心的单视界正则黑洞解（视界位于核心外或与核心重合）；分析其物质场、能量条件、热力学及标量扰动下的有效势函数。

Result: 1) 证明正则点状中心要求度规函数f具有偶数个变号零点（即偶数个视界）；2) 构造出两类单视界RBH解；3) 消除内柯西视界，从而避免经典质量膨胀；4) 证明其标量扰动有效势非负，故模式稳定。

Conclusion: 正则性本身不强制要求双视界结构；单视界、有限面积核心的正则黑洞是自洽且稳定的，为构建规避内视界不稳定的黑洞模型提供了新路径。

Abstract: Regular black holes (RBHs) are often constructed with a regular point-like center and two horizons, including an inner Cauchy horizon susceptible to mass inflation. We show that a two-horizon structure is not a necessary consequence of regularity. For static, spherically symmetric geometries with an asymptotically static exterior, we establish a horizon-parity theorem: a regular point-like center requires an even number of sign-changing zeros of $f$. We then construct two classes of single-horizon RBHs with a finite-area core, distinguished by whether the horizon lies outside the core or coincides with it, and analyze their matter content, energy conditions, and thermodynamic properties. The absence of an inner Cauchy horizon eliminates the classical mass-inflation mechanism on the single-horizon patch. We further establish mode stability against scalar perturbations for these finite-area-core configurations by showing that the effective potential is non-negative. Our results establish how the central geometry and asymptotic structure constrain horizon multiplicity and identify single-horizon configurations that avoid the classical inner-horizon instability.

</details>


### [141] [Late-time echoes from planar wave-packet scattering by an ultracompact star](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04525)
*Shanshan Deng,Jian-hua He*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过时域多极模式求和方法模拟了超紧凑无事件视界恒星中平面标量波的传播与散射，发现其早期响应类似黑洞环振，晚期则出现由内部稳定光圈捕获并泄漏产生的延迟回声；尽管各多极模回声规律，相干叠加后波形复杂不规则，但仍具时间组织性；最后证明第三代引力波探测器可分辨这些弱晚期信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超紧凑、无事件视界的恒星（如超致密星）对引力波信号的影响，以区分其与黑洞的观测特征，检验广义相对论及致密天体物态方程。

Method: 采用时域多极模式求和方法，模拟入射平面标量波在恒定密度超紧凑无视界星体时空中的传播与散射，并重建相干叠加的时域波形，与施瓦西黑洞响应对比；辅以信噪比分析评估可观测性。

Result: 早期响应近似黑洞环振（源于外部不稳定光球散射）；晚期出现延迟回声序列（源于内部稳定光圈捕获与外部势垒泄漏）；多极模回声规律但相干叠加导致波形不规则，仍具交替峰间距的时间结构；第三代探测器在假设应变归一化下可分辨该弱晚期信号。

Conclusion: 超紧凑无视界星体可产生具有时间组织性的晚期回声信号，虽波形复杂，但有望被第三代引力波探测器识别，为探测非黑洞致密天体提供新途径。

Abstract: We simulate the propagation and scattering of an incident plane scalar wave in the spacetime of an ultracompact, horizonless constant-density star using a time-domain multipole mode-sum method. By reconstructing the coherently superposed time-domain waveforms, we compare the star's dynamical response with that of a Schwarzschild black hole. We find that the early-time response closely mimics a black-hole ringdown due to wave scattering near the exterior unstable photon sphere. Conversely, the late-time signal develops a sequence of delayed echoes originating from wave trapping near the interior stable light ring and subsequent leakage through the exterior potential barrier. While individual multipoles exhibit regular echoes, their coherent superposition induces interference, producing complex waveforms with irregular profiles that nonetheless retain an underlying temporal organization with alternating peak separations. Finally, as a proof of concept, we perform a signal-to-noise ratio analysis using an assumed strain normalization, demonstrating that these weak, late-time signatures can be resolved by third-generation gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>


### [142] [On mass inflation and late-time divergences in limiting-curvature regular black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04618)
*Johanna Borissova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非极端正则黑洞中质量膨胀现象的瞬态特性及其后续的模型依赖性非指数晚期发散行为，基于广义Ori模型分析了几何机制，并提出了一类广义Fan-Wang正则黑洞以缓和质量膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非极端正则黑洞内视界处物理量发散的几何机制，并缓解质量膨胀这一可能破坏经典广义相对论预测的现象。

Method: 采用广义Ori模型，结合具有de Sitter核、Misner-Sharp质量密度与Kretschmann标量有m无关上界的静态球对称渐近平直正则黑洞模型，分析扰动时空内视界处的发散行为及质量参数演化。

Result: 发现内视界处的发散要求壳内质量参数超出正实数域，或导致静态度规的动力学延拓；引入可调的分数幂律晚期发散形式，可有效抑制质量膨胀，并筛选出一类广义Fan-Wang正则黑洞。

Conclusion: 质量膨胀在某些正则黑洞中可能是暂时的，晚期行为高度依赖模型构造；通过约束晚期发散形式，可获得物理上更自洽的黑洞解。

Abstract: Mass inflation in non-extremal regular black holes with a non-linear dependence of the Misner-Sharp mass $M$ on the asymptotic mass $m>0$ may be a transient phenomenon followed by a model-dependent non-exponential late-time divergence. We analyse these regimes based on the generalised Ori model for a class of static spherically symmetric and asymptotically flat regular black holes with a de Sitter core and an $m$-independent upper bound on the Misner-Sharp mass density and Kretschmann scalar inspired by quasi-topological gravity. We discuss the geometric mechanism behind divergences of physical quantities at the inner horizon of the perturbed spacetime, and show that these require the unphysical mass parameter inside the shell to evolve outside the positive-real domain, or in some cases force a distinct type of dynamical continuation of the static metric. Imposing an asymptotic late-time divergence in form of a fractional power-law scaling, tunable to be arbitrarily mild within the considered approximation and effectively taming mass inflation, allows us to single out a family of generalised Fan-Wang regular black holes.

</details>


### [143] [Gravitational-wave lensing beyond the diffraction integral: proper-time methods and the bound lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05152)
*Ginevra Braga,Raul Jimenez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了引力波在强场和弱场条件下的透镜效应，指出传统几何光学和衍射积分方法的局限性，并通过世界线（proper-time）表示法推导出更普适的波光学处理框架；特别分析了点质量透镜与束缚透镜（如绕行源）两种情形，揭示现有近似在相位、振幅及时间延迟建模中的系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引力波透镜理论依赖于eikonal、paraxial和薄透镜等多重近似，但这些近似在强场、大角度或动态源（如绕行透镜的波源）情形下失效；本文旨在建立一个不依赖这些近似、基于量子类比（世界线路径积分）的自洽波光学框架。

Method: 采用辅助固有时（proper time）将标量波方程精确一阶化，把频率类比为1/ℏ；利用Duru-Kleinert规范与Kustaanheimo-Stiefel变换求得点质量透镜的精确传播子；对束缚透镜情形，引入各世界线独立的推迟时间与多普勒因子，构建时域精确信号模型。

Result: 对点质量透镜，严格导出标准放大因子，确认其有效性源于库仑势本质而非近似；对束缚透镜，发现传统Shapiro相位、几何光学、抛物近似均严重失准：几何光学漏掉46–100%的透镜效应，静态放大因子在近合点误差达(1–2)wM/R，且完全忽略回波信号。

Conclusion: 引力波透镜必须采用基于世界线的波光学方法；传统近似仅在特定参数域成立，动态透镜系统需完整时域建模；该框架为未来LISA等探测器识别复杂透镜信号提供理论基础。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) can have wavelengths comparable to the Schwarzschild radius of their lenses, and detectors record their phase, so lensing requires wave optics. Its standard tool, the diffraction integral, follows from the particle path integral of a scalar wave on a static weak field, under the eikonal (one-way) and paraxial approximations, plus the thin-lens limit. We give a self-contained account of how the proper-time (worldline) representation overcomes these steps. An auxiliary proper time makes the wave equation exactly first order, with frequency as $1/\hbar$, so geometric optics and, for paraxial thin-lens paths, the diffraction integral follow from stationarity of an exact integral. For a point mass, the Duru-Kleinert (DK) gauge and Kustaanheimo-Stiefel map give the exact propagator; for a distant observer the DK time and fibre angle become the radius and azimuth of a lens plane, the result is the point-lens amplification factor with the offset set by the excess path, and geometric optics requires $w\gg1$ and $wy\gg1$. This agreement reflects the Coulomb potential, not the approximations: the exact eikonal condition, $\varepsilon_E\simeq\barλ/d+GM/c^2d+\mathcal O(\sin^2θ/2)$, fails near the lens and at large angles, and the one-way step is a non-relativistic limit. We then treat the bound lens, a GW source orbiting its lens, which emits a backscattered echo and moves during the lensing delays. Its exact time-domain signal gives each worldline its own retarded time and Doppler factor, so no single amplification factor describes it. In the diffraction regime the Shapiro phase alone misses all lensing beyond it, geometric optics misses 46-100% of it near conjunction at $w=0.3$, the paraxial geometry fails on the far side, and the static amplification factor along the orbit errs by $\approx(1-2)\,wM/R$ near conjunction and by the echo amplitude elsewhere.

</details>


### [144] [On Isaacson's Procedure in $f(R)$-Gravity with Second Order Metric Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04639)
*Petr V. Tretyakov,Alexander N. Petrov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在f(R)引力理论中，考虑度规张量二阶分解项对高频率引力波平均能量-动量张量反作用效应的可能贡献；结果表明，在德西特背景上，这些二阶项的贡献被强烈压制，因此先前工作（arXiv:2504.02253）的结果保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验先前关于f(R)引力中引力波背反应工作的完备性，特别是考察被忽略的度规二阶扰动项是否会影响已得结论。

Method: 在de Sitter背景上，将度规张量展开至二阶，系统分析二阶项对平均能量-动量张量背反应的贡献，并进行数量级估计与抑制机制分析。

Result: 在de Sitter背景下，所有来自度规二阶分解项的贡献均被强烈压制，不改变原有一阶分析所得结论。

Conclusion: arXiv:2504.02253中基于一阶度规分解得到的f(R)引力中引力波背反应结果是稳健且可靠的，无需引入二阶修正。

Abstract: In the recent paper arXiv:2504.02253 we generalized classical Isaacson's procedure for describing back-reaction of the averaged energy-momentum for high frequency gravitational waves to $f(R)$ gravity case with a choice of the de Sitter background. To describe this second order effect we used standard first order decomposition of metric tensor. In this paper, we take into account possible contribution to this process from second order term in metric tensor decomposition. We find that on the de Sitter background all possible contributions from this term are strongly suppressed and therefore results obtained in the paper arXiv:2504.02253 are left unchanged.

</details>


### [145] [Final state of superradiant instability in Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04646)
*Afsaneh Rahmani,Samaneh Oghbaiee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带电黑洞在Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton理论与无质量带电标量场下的不稳定性，发现其在特定频率范围内失稳并形成标量‘毛’，存在纯带电黑洞到带毛黑洞的相变，相变临界温度随黑洞尺寸增大而升高，且系统在临界温度达到稳定平衡态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究带电黑洞在更广义引力理论（如Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton）中的不稳定性机制，特别是标量‘毛’的形成与非线性超辐射过程的关系。

Method: 基于Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton理论，引入无质量带电标量场，通过数值积分求解耦合场方程，分析黑洞稳定性及相变行为。

Result: 发现存在一个临界温度T_C：当T < T_C时，系统演化为带毛黑洞；当T > T_C时保持纯带电黑洞；T_C随黑洞尺度增大而升高；系统在T_C处达到稳定平衡，标志着超辐射不稳定性终结。

Conclusion: 超辐射不稳定性可驱动带电黑洞发生相变并产生标量毛，该过程受黑洞尺度与温度调控；结果有助于理解非线性超辐射演化及标量化黑洞在扩展引力理论中的起源。

Abstract: This article examines the instability of charged black holes, using Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory and a massless charged scalar field. Our study demonstrates that these black holes become unstable in a certain range of frequencies, which causes scalar "hair" to form outside the event horizon. By using numerical integration on the coupled field equations, we show that there is a phase transition between a purely charged black hole and a hairy black hole configuration. The results show that the hairy phase appears when the temperature is less than $T_C$, the critical temperature, whereas for $T>T_C$, the system stays purely charged. Also, our findings show that the critical temperature increases with the size of the black hole, implying that the onset of the phase transition becomes progressively more difficult for larger horizons. Lastly, we see that the system enters a stable equilibrium at the critical temperature, which is the final state of the superradiant instability. These results provide us with useful information on how nonlinear superradiant events change and how scalarized black holes arise in more comprehensive theories of gravity.

</details>


### [146] [Particle dynamics in rotating charged Bumblebee black holes: An analytical correspondence with Kerr-Newman](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04750)
*Deyou Chen,Chuang Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了无自旋和有自旋测试粒子在缓慢旋转带电Einstein-Bumblebee黑洞时空赤道面动力学与有效Kerr-Newman时空之间的解析对应关系；该对应在Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon框架下、通过适当参数匹配实现，但仅部分保持动力学性质（如轨道半径和角速度相同），而径向动力学和不稳定性率则受洛伦兹破缺影响产生标度变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究洛伦兹破缺背景下（Einstein-Bumblebee理论）黑洞周围粒子运动能否用标准广义相对论中熟知的Kerr-Newman时空有效描述，从而界定其适用范围与局限性。

Method: 基于Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon方程研究自旋粒子在缓慢旋转带电Einstein-Bumblebee黑洞赤道面的圆轨道及径向扰动；通过参数匹配建立与有效Kerr-Newman时空的解析对应，并比较轨道几何、运动学量（如坐标角速度）及动力学行为（如径向不稳定性率、轨迹演化）。

Result: 发现对应圆轨道具有相同半径和坐标角速度；径向动力学和局部不稳定性率呈现洛伦兹破缺依赖的标度律；但完整轨道轨迹及一个方位周期内的径向扰动增长并不相同。

Conclusion: 有效Kerr-Newman对应仅能部分再现原始Einstein-Bumblebee黑洞的动力学，它保留了某些运动学特征，却无法刻画由洛伦兹破缺引起的径向结构变形所导致的动力学修正；因此该对应具有明确的适用边界。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish an analytical correspondence between the equatorial dynamics of spinless and spinning test particles in a slowly rotating charged Einstein--Bumblebee black hole spacetime and those in an effective Kerr--Newman spacetime. For spinning particles, the correspondence is established within the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon framework under appropriate parameter matching. We show that corresponding circular orbits have identical radii and coordinate angular velocities, while their radial dynamics and local instability rates exhibit a Lorentz-violation-dependent scaling. Nevertheless, the correspondence does not imply complete dynamical equivalence, as orbital trajectories and radial perturbation growth over an azimuthal period generally differ. These results delineate the scope and limitations of the effective Kerr--Newman correspondence by distinguishing the dynamical properties preserved under parameter matching from those modified by the Lorentz-violating radial deformation.

</details>


### [147] [On the Motion of a Point Electric Charge in the Field of Wu-Yang Monopoles in Bertotti-Robinson Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04816)
*Tekin Dereli,Yorgo Senikoglu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有非最小RF²型曲率-杨-米尔斯场耦合的SU(2)爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯系统，发现Bertotti-Robinson几何AdS₂×S²可精确容纳Wu-Yang磁单极解，且耦合仅重正化S²半径；同时给出了电荷在此背景下的解析运动轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非最小曲率-规范场耦合下引力与规范场相互作用的新精确解，特别是Wu-Yang磁单极在弯曲时空中的实现及其物理效应。

Method: 采用外微分形式和拉格朗日乘子法约束列维-奇维塔联络，分析SU(2)爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯系统；利用Hopf纤维化将Wu-Yang场投影为Dirac型磁场，并求解点电荷运动方程。

Result: 证明AdS₂×S²时空可精确支持Wu-Yang磁单极解，非最小耦合仅改变S²半径；给出非共形平直分支解；导出电荷在该磁场中沿圆锥面振荡运动的闭式解。

Conclusion: Wu-Yang磁单极在含RF²耦合的爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯理论中具有稳健性，其几何实现与电荷动力学展现出清晰的解析结构，揭示了非最小耦合对规范场时空构型的关键调控作用。

Abstract: We study the $SU(2)$ Einstein-Yang-Mills system with non-minimal $RF^2$-type couplings of Yang-Mills fields to curvature, formulated with exterior differential forms and a Lagrange-multiplier constraint for the Levi-Civita connection. We show that the conformally flat Bertotti-Robinson geometry $AdS_2\times S^2$ carries the Wu-Yang magnetic pole as an exact solution for arbitrary non-minimal couplings; the couplings only renormalise the radius of the $S^2$. The non-conformally-flat branch of solutions known for this system is recalled. The Hopf fibration $S^3/S^2\cong S^1$ of the gauge group manifold projects the Wu-Yang field on a uniform Dirac-type magnetic field, and the motion of a point electric charge in it is solved in closed form: the charge moves on a cone and oscillates between the horizon and a maximal radius.

</details>


### [148] [Mixed-Order Parity-Violating Corrections to Rotating Black Holes in Combined Scalar Gauss-Bonnet and Dynamical Chern-Simons Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04945)
*Abhishek Rout,Brett Altschul*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了在标量高斯-博内与动力学陈-西蒙斯有效理论联合框架下，旋转黑洞的混合阶O(ζ_GB ζ_CS)修正解，并通过伪谱法求解耦合的标量场与度规方程，计算了多种黑洞物理可观测量随自旋的变化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究同时包含宇称偶（高斯-博内）和宇称奇（陈-西蒙斯）引力修正的混合效应，弥补以往仅研究单一边界修正的不足，检验其对黑洞结构和可观测量的独特影响。

Method: 采用伪谱数值方法求解耦合的标量场与爱因斯坦场方程，构造满足全部场方程（包括未显式施加的约束方程）的混合阶微扰解；在固定质量与角动量下，系统计算从视界到光子环的一系列可观测量，并评估数值误差与理论截断不确定性。

Result: 获得首个满足全部场方程的O(ζ_GB ζ_CS)混合黑洞解；发现多个视界量（角速度、表面引力、面积、Wald熵）及轨道/类波数量（如ISCO半径、光子环半径、ringdown估计）均随自旋变化出现符号翻转；Wald熵严格满足热力学第一定律（精度达10^{-5}）；混合系数的自旋依赖性明显区别于纯GB或纯CS单一边界情形。

Conclusion: 混合高阶修正产生不可忽略且定性新颖的物理效应，不能由单一边界修正简单叠加预测；该结果为未来引力波与EHT观测区分不同量子引力有效理论提供了关键基准。

Abstract: We construct the leading mixed-order $\mathcal{O}(ζ_{\rm GB}ζ_{\rm CS})$ deformation of rotating black holes in a combined scalar Gauss-Bonnet and dynamical Chern-Simons effective theory, the sector in which the parity-even and parity-odd corrections act simultaneously. Using a pseudospectral solution of the coupled mixed scalar and metric equations, we compute horizon and equatorial-geodesic observables--the horizon angular velocity, surface gravity, area, and Wald entropy; the innermost stable circular orbit and photon-ring radii; and photon-ring eikonal estimates of the ringdown--at fixed mass and angular momentum over the spin range $0.1\le a\le0.9$, where the solution is numerically convergent and the perturbative expansion is controlled for sufficiently small dimensionless couplings. We verify the exact Kerr and Schwarzschild limits, reproduce available pure-sector benchmarks, and quantify spectral, residual, and extraction uncertainties. The mixed solution satisfies every component of the field equations, including the one that is not imposed in its construction, and its Wald entropy satisfies the first law to about one part in $10^{5}$. The mixed coefficients exhibit spin dependence qualitatively distinct from the individual sectors within this EFT truncation. All four horizon quantities, and several orbital and eikonal ones, change sign as functions of spin. The ringdown results are leading photon-ring eikonal estimates; a full coupled finite-$\ell$ perturbation calculation is left to future work.

</details>


### [149] [Scale-Dependent Weak-Field Dynamics in Horndeski Theory: Galaxy Rotation Curves and Turnaround Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05089)
*Hatice Özer*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Horndeski引力理论中一类与引力波兼容的弱场动力学，重点分析其在星系及星系际尺度上的效应，发现标量场会改变轨道动力学、旋转速度径向依赖关系，并导致动力学质量与透镜质量之间的引力滑移；在大尺度上导出了由有效引力与背景曲率平衡决定的‘转折尺度’。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Horndeski引力理论中与引力波观测兼容的子类在星系和宇宙学尺度上的可观测弱场效应，特别是标量场非线性导数相互作用带来的尺度依赖性修正。

Method: 在稳态、近似球对称假设下，采用弱场近似方法，保留标量场非线性导数相互作用项，分别分析星系尺度（通过有效势、圆轨道稳定性、旋转曲线、动力学/透镜质量关系）和星系际尺度（渐近弱场、转折尺度推导）的动力学行为。

Result: 星系尺度上：标量场修改轨道动力学与旋转速度分布，并产生非零引力滑移（即动力学质量 ≠ 透镜质量）；星系际尺度上：导出由有效引力与空间曲率竞争决定的转折半径，大距离处引力场由背景曲率主导。

Conclusion: 该Horndeski子类在弱场下可产生显著且可观测的尺度依赖引力修正，既影响星系动力学又塑造大尺度结构边界，为区分修改引力与暗物质模型提供了新理论依据。

Abstract: In this work, the weak-field dynamics of a gravitational-wave compatible subclass of Horndeski gravity is investigated on galactic and intergalactic scales. The analysis is performed within the weak-field approximation for stationary and approximately spherically symmetric configurations, retaining the nonlinear scalar derivative interactions responsible for scale-dependent modifications of the gravitational field. On galactic scales, the transition regime is investigated through the effective potential, the stability of circular orbits, galaxy rotation curves, and the relation between dynamical and lensing masses. The analysis shows that the scalar field modifies the orbital dynamics and the radial dependence of the rotational velocity, while generating a nonvanishing gravitational slip between the dynamical and lensing mass estimates. On intergalactic scales, the asymptotic weak-field dynamics is investigated and a turnaround scale is derived from the balance between the effective gravitational attraction and the large-scale curvature contribution. At large distances, the asymptotic gravitational field is governed by the background curvature contribution.

</details>


### [150] [Excitation factors and exceptional points of Kerr--Newman quasinormal modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05201)
*Jun-Kun Zhao,Tieguang Zi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kerr-Newman黑洞的准正规模（QNMs）的激发因子和例外点（EPs），利用精确分离的引力-电磁方程，发现激发因子随自旋和电荷变化呈现不同行为，并识别出42个EPs，揭示了旋转与电荷对模式激发强度及非厄米谱中模式合并模式的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Kerr-Newman黑洞中不同准正规模的相对激发强度及其在非厄米谱中的共alescence行为，特别是旋转和电荷如何影响这些特性。

Method: 使用精确分离的gravitoelectromagnetic方程分析Kerr-Newman黑洞的准正规模，计算激发因子并识别例外点（EPs）。

Result: 发现引力主导模式的激发因子幅值随自旋增加而减小；电磁主导模式则呈现单调与非单调行为；首阶泛音激发因子可与基模相当甚至更大；共识别出42个例外点，部分形成按泛音序排列的序列，其余例外点具有相同模式标签但出现在不同自旋与电荷参数下。

Conclusion: 旋转和电荷共同塑造了Kerr-Newman黑洞中不同准正规模的激发强度分布及例外点结构，为理解其非厄米动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study excitation factors and investigate exceptional points (EPs) of Kerr--Newman quasinormal modes using the exact separated gravitoelectromagnetic equations. At fixed charge, the magnitudes of the excitation factors for the gravitational-led modes studied here decrease with increasing spin, whereas those for electromagnetic-led modes exhibit both monotonic and nonmonotonic behavior. The magnitude of the excitation factor for the first overtone can be comparable to or larger than that for the fundamental mode. We identify 42 EPs at which pairs of modes coalesce in the gravitational-led and electromagnetic-led sectors. Some EPs form sequences involving successive overtone pairs, with higher-overtone pairs coalescing progressively closer to the extremal limit. The additional EPs identified outside these sequences all share the same mode-pair labels as sequence members, but occur at different values of the black hole spin and charge. These findings clarify how rotation and charge shape the relative excitation strengths of different modes and the pattern of mode coalescence in the non-Hermitian spectrum of Kerr--Newman black holes.

</details>


### [151] [Full-Stokes response to a stochastic gravitational-wave background in pulsar timing arrays: Fully analytic overlap reduction functions at finite distance and coherence of close pulsar pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05219)
*Kun Zhou,Jin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了在广义相对论框架下，脉冲星计时阵列对各向异性张量引力波背景的全斯托克斯响应的完全解析重叠还原函数，保留了脉冲星距离及其完整传播相位，并分析了不同极矩、极化分量及误差影响下的响应特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确建模脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）对各向异性引力波背景的响应，需严格考虑脉冲星距离、传播相位及多种极化模式，以提升引力波探测灵敏度与系统误差控制能力。

Method: 推导全斯托克斯响应的解析重叠还原函数；按天球多极展开（至ℓ=8）；区分地球项与脉冲星项；分析强度、圆偏振、线偏振（电/磁）分量的数学形式（含指数积分、对数、初等函数）；研究对称性导致的精确零点与传播抬升的地球项零点；引入均匀近似连接长/短波极限；进行高斯微分平均与天空积分验证。

Result: 获得各向异性张量GW背景下的全解析ORF；发现即使无偏振背景也可产生虚部交叉谱；揭示脉冲星项在2πfd/c≲1时仍可保持相干；给出共距误差抵消与相对深度误差影响 coherence 的定量关系；建立极化协方差界与相对深度噪声预算；通过实际PTA构型验证距离精度、带宽和计时拟合的影响。

Conclusion: 该解析框架显著提升了PTA对各向异性引力波背景的建模精度与物理可解释性，为未来高精度引力波天文学提供了关键理论工具与误差控制指南。

Abstract: We derive fully analytic overlap reduction functions for the full-Stokes response of a pulsar timing array to an anisotropic tensor gravitational-wave background in general relativity. The response retains both pulsar distances and their complete propagation phases. At each fixed finite sky multipole, the intensity and circular polarization responses reduce to finite combinations involving exponential integrals and logarithms, while the electric and magnetic linear polarization responses require only elementary functions. We evaluate all components through $\ell=8$. Symmetry separates exact nulls from additional Earth-term nulls that propagation can lift. Even an unpolarized background can produce an imaginary cross-spectrum. For a pair with three-dimensional separation $d$, the pulsar terms can remain coherent when $2πfd/c\lesssim1$, even if both pulsars are many wavelengths from Earth. A uniform approximation retaining all four Earth and pulsar terms connects this regime to the short-wavelength limit, and a rotationally invariant relation fixes the leading transverse magnetic response. Common-distance errors cancel from the aligned pair phase, while relative-depth errors directly affect coherence. Under Gaussian differential averaging, fixed low-multipole norms and the squared norm summed over all multipoles decay at different rates as the response shifts to higher multipoles. Published pulsar configurations illustrate the roles of distance accuracy, bandwidth, and timing fits. At fixed pointwise intensity, a polarization covariance bound and a relative-depth noise budget quantify when differencing suppresses the background. Independent sky integration and matched-accuracy benchmarks test the analytic expressions over the parameter ranges studied.

</details>


### [152] [Dirac stars in Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05240)
*Salvatore Capozziello,Allah Ditta,M. Yousaf,G. Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在爱因斯坦引力与欧拉-海森堡非线性电动力学耦合下，带电经典旋量场构成的自引力星体（Dirac星），发现电荷和四次自相互作用显著影响其质量、尺寸、致密性和最大稳定质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性电动力学（特别是欧拉-海森堡模型）与带电旋量场结合时对自引力星体结构与稳定性的影响，弥补现有Dirac星研究中忽略高阶电磁效应的不足。

Method: 建立带U(1)规范耦合与四次自相互作用的旋量场-引力-EH电动力学耦合模型；对静态球对称构型推导场方程，化为无量纲本征值问题，并数值求解。

Result: 电荷提升星体频率、质量和半径，最大质量从0.709增至1.027（单位M_Pl²/μ）；四次项削弱内部电场，使同电荷星体更致密、束缚更强，但最大质量反降低9%（如耦合0.6时从0.924降至0.840）；弱场下质量修正极小，大耦合对应有效非线性理论而非微扰QED；所有解满足全部能量条件且无事件视界。

Conclusion: 欧拉-海森堡非线性效应对带电Dirac星的结构与稳定性具有定性影响：虽增强单星参数，却降低整体稳定性极限；该模型在强场下应视为有效非线性电动力学框架，而非QED微扰展开。

Abstract: We study self-gravitating configurations of a charged classical spinor field coupled to gravity and to Euler-Heisenberg (EH) nonlinear electrodynamics, truncated at quartic order. The spinor is gauged under a U(1) field and may carry a quartic self-interaction. For static and spherically symmetric configurations we derive the field equations, reduce them to a dimensionless eigenvalue problem for the spinor frequency and solve it numerically. In the uncharged limit our solutions coincide with the known Dirac stars of general relativity. The electric charge increases the frequency, the mass and the size of the stars, and the maximal mass grows from $0.709$ to $1.027$ (in units of $M_{\rm Pl}^2/μ$) when the charge coupling rises from zero to $0.75$. The quartic term changes the relation between the electric field and the enclosed charge, so that the field inside the star is weaker than in the Maxwell case. Our central result concerns stars with the same number of fermions, and therefore the same total charge. For them the quartic term changes the mass by less than $0.2\%$, but it makes the stars more compact and more strongly bound and shifts the turning point of the family to a smaller particle number. This shift is what lowers the maximal mass, by $9\%$ (from $0.924$ to $0.840$) at charge coupling $0.6$ as the dimensionless EH coupling goes from zero to $200$. In the weak-field regime of quantum electrodynamics (QED) the relative mass correction is $-1.5\times10^{-5}(E_{\max}/E_c)^2$, so the larger couplings describe the quartic model as an effective nonlinear electrodynamics rather than a controlled QED correction. All solutions satisfy the null, weak, strong and dominant energy conditions, show a positive pressure anisotropy, and remain free of horizons. Their stability is discussed through the binding energy and the turning point of the mass curve.

</details>


### [153] [Kerr metric perturbations in Lorenz gauge via numerical relativity methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05259)
*Nami Nishimura,Nils L. Vu,Adrià Gispert-Fernández,Cillian Kelly,Lawrence E. Kidder,Benjamin Leather,Thomas Osburn,Jonathan E. Thompson,Samuel D. Upton,Barry Wardell*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数值相对论的新计算方法，首次实现了在高速自转克尔黑洞时空（自旋达0.998M）中对圆轨道的指数收敛一阶引力自作用计算，并为后续二阶计算奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为准确预测不对称质量致密双星产生的引力波信号（尤其是LISA任务所需），需在克尔黑洞时空中进行至少二阶质量比微扰的引力自作用计算；但此前仅在非自转的史瓦西黑洞中实现，因爱因斯坦方程的非线性复杂性，在克尔情形下长期未突破。

Method: 采用基于数值相对论的新方法：将洛伦兹规范下的微扰爱因斯坦方程按方位角m模在频域展开，用谱间断伽辽金离散、自适应网格加密和预处理迭代求解器求解所得椭圆型偏微分方程。

Result: 实现了克尔时空中圆赤道轨道的一阶引力自作用的指数收敛数值计算，最高可达黑洞自旋|a|/M = 0.998（索恩极限）；所获一阶数据精度高、行为良好，可直接用于构建二阶非线性源项及求解二阶度规微扰。

Conclusion: 该方法成功突破了克尔时空高阶自作用计算的关键瓶颈，为LISA引力波建模提供了首个可行的全广义相对论高精度框架，并天然可拓展至二阶微扰计算。

Abstract: Gravitational self-force calculations are needed to accurately describe the motion of a small body in a spinning (Kerr) black hole spacetime, which is vital to predict gravitational wave signals from asymmetric-mass compact binaries. Accurate calculations for the upcoming LISA mission require a minimum of second perturbative order in the mass ratio, which has thus far only been achieved for nonspinning (Schwarzschild) black holes due to nonlinear complexities of the Einstein equations. In this work, we calculate Kerr metric perturbations with a new computational approach based on numerical relativity methods, extending recent scalar self-force results to full general relativity. We formulate the perturbative Lorenz-gauge Einstein equations in terms of azimuthal $m$-modes in the frequency domain and solve the resulting elliptic partial differential equations using a spectral discontinuous Galerkin discretization, adaptive mesh refinement, and preconditioned iterative solvers. With our new method, we achieve exponentially convergent first-order gravitational self-force calculations for circular equatorial orbits in Kerr spacetime up to spin $|a|/M=0.998$ (the Thorne limit). Our framework is designed to carry over to second order by delivering accurate and well-behaved first-order data for nonlinear source construction, and as a solver for the second-order metric perturbations themselves.

</details>
